论文对照阅读

达尔文–哥德尔机：自改进智能体的开放式演化

J. Zhang et al.

导读 正文 评论

正式阅读前 · 先结论，再定点深挖

全文导读

这篇是自改进智能体论文，容易被收成「又一个会改自己代码的智能体」。下面先给出主张、机制、证据与精读路径。读完这一页，再带着一个具体问题进入正文。

一句话主张。 DGM 用经验验证代替哥德尔机的形式证明：智能体改自己的代码，对照编码基准打分，并把全部版本放进档案做开放式探索；这是昂贵的离线演化，不是修 issue 时当场改脚手架。

和 AI 工程、智能体的关系

这篇首先是离线自改进 / harness 搜索：基础模型权重冻住，改的是工具、提示与工作流，写在智能体自己的 Python 仓库里。对 AI 工程的用处可以收成一句：与其人手穷举「最优脚手架」，不如把改脚手架本身变成可评估的编码任务，并用档案保住那些当下不最优、日后能当踏脚石的版本。

它和 LIVE-SWE-AGENT 不是同一条路。DGM 在基准上离线改自己的脚手架，一轮 SWE-bench 据原文约 2.2 万美元、约两周；学完往往绑在这次搜索用的模型与基准上，部署时脚手架已经冻住。LIVE-SWE-AGENT 把演化挪到当前这条轨迹里，离线成本为零。和 Cordiverse 的关系见读后评论：档案能组合出新智能体，卸载与依赖语义还没有。

问题从哪来

理论哥德尔机要求每次自修改都可证有益；实践里证不了。元学习能搜算法，但搜索空间仍由人划。人手打造的智能体（提示、思维链、工具、记忆）把能力锁在设计者的巧思之内。ADAS 用固定元智能体生成下游智能体——下游变强，不会让「改别人的那位」变强。

作者的切口是：把自改进定义成「修改自己仓库」的编码任务，于是基准涨分被当成更会改自己的证据；再用达尔文式档案，避免只盯最近一版而卡在局部最优。

机制（档案 + 自修改 + 经验评估）

从档案里抽父代，让它改自己

初始化只有一个瘦基座：Bash + 整文件编辑。每轮按「分数高、子代少」抽样，人人留非零概率。父代看自己的失败日志，实现下一特征，写进自己的代码库。

能改代码的才进档，分数用基准估

新智能体先过「还能编辑仓库」的门，再分阶段上 10 / 50 / 200 题。档案不删旧节点：掉过分的分枝后来仍可能长出冠军。开放式环本身冻住，DGM 改不了。

两条消融钉死构件：元智能体冻住（DGM w/o self-improve）早期涨、很快平台；不建档案、只改最近一版（DGM w/o open-ended）几乎走不动。贪心「只从第一名分枝」也明显弱于完整开放式。

作者实际贡献了什么

  • 经验版哥德尔机：用基准验证代替形式证明，并闭合「下游变强 → 更会改自己」的自指环。
  • 开放式档案：全部有效子代进档；踏脚石可以很晚才兑现（如图 3 节点 24）。
  • 数字：SWE-bench 20.0% → 50.0%；完整 Polyglot 14.2% → 30.7%；开源核验 SoTA 子集约 51%，DGM 最佳约 50%。
  • 迁移：换基础模型、换基准、换语言仍涨；只在 Python 上搜会伤到其他语言。
  • 安全与代价：沙箱、时限、可追溯档案；单次 SWE-bench 约 2.2 万美元。附录 H 还展示目标黑客。

章节地图：去哪找什么

读正文时的一条纪律

每一轮精读前，先写下这一轮只想搞清的一句话（例如：「档案为什么必须留下掉分的节点」「2.2 万美元买到的是一次搜索还是一个可在线改的智能体」）。读完能用白话复述，这一轮就算过关；复述不出，再回到该页三列，而不是从头重读。

英文「文本抽取」列支持划词查词（离线词库）。正文工具栏可开关列：先只开「白话解读」扫地图，再打开译文或 PDF 对读。

第 1 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 DARWIN GÖDEL MACHINE: OPEN-ENDED EVOLUTION OF SELF-IMPROVING AGENTS Jenny Zhang*,1,2 Shengran Hu*,1,2,3 Cong Lu1,2,3 Robert Lange†,3 Jeff Clune†,1,2,4 1University of British Columbia 2Vector Institute 3Sakana AI 4Canada CIFAR AI Chair {jennyzzt,srhu,conglu}@cs.ubc.ca, robert@sakana.ai, jeff.clune@ubc.ca ABSTRACT Most of today’s AI systems are constrained by human-designed, fixed architectures and cannot autonomously and continuously improve themselves. The scientific method, on the other hand, is a cumulative and open-ended system, where each innovation builds upon previous artifacts, enabling future discoveries. There is growing hope that the current manual process of advancing AI could itself be automated. If done safely, such automation would accelerate AI development and allow us to reap its benefits much sooner. This prospect raises the question of how AI systems can endlessly improve themselves while getting better at solving relevant problems. Meta-learning can automate the discovery of novel algorithms, but is limited by first-order improvements and the human design of a suitable search space. The Gödel machine (Schmidhuber, 2007) proposed a theoretical alternative: a self-improving AI that repeatedly modifies itself in a provably beneficial manner. Unfortunately, proving that most changes are net beneficial is impossible in practice. We introduce the Darwin Gödel Machine (DGM), a novel self-improving system that iteratively modifies its own code (thereby also improving its ability to modify its own codebase) and empirically validates each change using coding benchmarks. Inspired by Darwinian evolution and open-endedness research, the DGM grows an archive of generated coding agents. It samples agents from this archive, which self-modify to create new, interesting versions of themselves. This open-ended exploration forms a growing tree of diverse, high-quality agents and allows the parallel exploration of many different paths through the search space. Empirically, the DGM automatically improves its coding capabilities (e.g., better code editing tools, long-context window management, peer-review mechanisms), increasing performance on SWE-bench from 20.0% to 50.0%, and on Polyglot from 14.2% to 30.7%. Furthermore, the DGM significantly outperforms baselines without selfimprovement or open-ended exploration. All experiments were done with safety precautions (e.g., sandboxing, human oversight). Overall, the DGM represents a significant step toward self-improving AI, capable of gathering its own stepping stones along a path that unfolds into endless innovation. All code is open-sourced at https://github.com/jennyzzt/dgm. INTRODUCTION Scientific progress is cumulative and open-ended, with each breakthrough standing on the shoulders of countless prior insights. In the same way, our most advanced AI systems are built upon a long lineage of innovations. For instance, transformers (Vaswani et al., 2017), the backbone of current large language models (LLMs) (Brown et al., 2020), did not emerge in isolation but were built upon years of past innovations, such as recurrent neural networks (Linnainmaa, 1970; Amari, 1972; Hopfield, 1982; Rumelhart et al., 1985) and attention mechanisms (Schmidhuber & Huber, 1990; Bahdanau et al., 2015; Kim et al., 2017; Parikh et al., 2016). However, most of today’s AI systems remain bound by fixed, human-designed architectures that learn within predefined boundaries, without the capacity to autonomously rewrite their own source code to self-improve. As a result, each advancement in AI development still leans heavily on human interventions, tethering the pace of progress. 发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

达尔文–哥德尔机：自改进智能体的开放式演化

DARWIN GÖDEL MACHINE: OPEN-ENDED EVOLUTION OF SELF-IMPROVING AGENTS

Jenny Zhang*,1,2 Shengran Hu*,1,2,3 Cong Lu1,2,3 Robert Lange†,3 Jeff Clune†,1,2,4

1University of British Columbia 2Vector Institute 3Sakana AI 4Canada CIFAR AI Chair

{jennyzzt,srhu,conglu}@cs.ubc.ca, robert@sakana.ai, jeff.clune@ubc.ca

摘要

当今绝大多数 AI 系统受限于人类设计的固定架构，无法自主、持续地改进自身。科学方法则相反：它是累积且开放式的，每一项创新都建立在既有成果之上，从而催生后续发现。人们越来越希望，目前靠人手推进 AI 的过程本身也能被自动化。若能安全地做到这一点，将加速 AI 发展，使我们更早收获其益处。由此引出的问题是：AI 系统如何在不断变得更擅长解决相关问题的同时，无尽地改进自身。元学习可以自动发现新算法，但受限于一阶改进，以及须由人设计合适的搜索空间。哥德尔机（Schmidhuber, 2007）提出了一种理论替代：一种自改进 AI，以可证明有益的方式反复修改自身。遗憾的是，在实践中几乎无法证明大多数改动净收益为正。我们提出 Darwin Gödel Machine（DGM）：一种新型自改进系统，迭代地修改自己的代码（从而也提升其修改自身代码库的能力），并用编码基准对每次改动做经验验证。受达尔文演化与开放式研究启发，DGM 生长一份已生成编码智能体的档案。它从档案中采样智能体，这些智能体再自修改，生成新的、有趣的自身版本。这种开放式探索形成一棵不断增长的、多样且高质量的智能体树，并允许在搜索空间中并行探索多条路径。经验上，DGM 自动提升了其编码能力（例如更好的代码编辑工具、长上下文窗口管理、同行评审机制），在 SWE-bench 上将性能从 20.0% 提升到 50.0%，在 Polyglot 上从 14.2% 提升到 30.7%。此外，DGM 显著优于没有自改进或没有开放式探索的基线。全部实验均采取安全措施（例如沙箱、人工监督）。总体而言，DGM 标志着迈向自改进 AI 的重要一步：它能沿一条展开为无尽创新的路径，自行收集踏脚石。全部代码开源于 https://github.com/jennyzzt/dgm。

引言

科学进步是累积且开放式的，每一次突破都站在无数先前洞见的肩膀上。同样，我们最先进的 AI 系统也建立在一条漫长的创新谱系之上。例如，作为当前大语言模型（LLM）（Brown et al., 2020）骨干的 Transformer（Vaswani et al., 2017），并非孤立出现，而是建立在循环神经网络（Linnainmaa, 1970; Amari, 1972; Hopfield, 1982; Rumelhart et al., 1985）与注意力机制（Schmidhuber & Huber, 1990; Bahdanau et al., 2015; Kim et al., 2017; Parikh et al., 2016）等多年成果之上。然而，当今大多数 AI 系统仍被固定的、人手设计的架构束缚，只在预定边界内学习，没有能力自主改写自己的源代码以自改进。因此，AI 发展的每一步推进仍严重依赖人工干预，拖住了进步的节奏。本文

白话解读

本页是封面、摘要和引言开头，先把全文主张钉死。人手焊死的 AI 架构不会自己改源码；理论哥德尔机要求「可证有益」的自修改，实践里证不了。DGM 改成用编码基准做经验验证，并像达尔文演化那样维护一份智能体档案，从中采样、自改、再评估。SWE-bench 从百分之二十升到五十，Polyglot 从十四点二升到三十点七。类比：不是先写完形式证明再改图纸，而是边改边用考卷打分，并把旧版本都存档当踏脚石。

第 2 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 investigates the intriguing possibility of safely automating the search for ever-better AI. One can imagine an AI system that, like scientific discovery itself, becomes an engine of its own advancement: building upon its past, recursively improving, and propelling itself toward more advanced capabilities. Archive New coding agent Coding agent Self-modify child Coding agent’s own repo + Self-improve instruction Code Diff: Feature to improve itself Coding agent select Task repo (e.g., GitHub repo) + Task instruction (e.g., GitHub issue) Code Diff: Solve task New coding agent Evaluate on benchmark add parent Figure 1: Darwin Gödel Machine. The DGM iteratively builds a growing archive of agents by interleaving self-modification with downstream task evaluation. Agents in the archive are selected for self-modification through open-ended exploration. Schmidhuber (2007) presented a class of mathematically rigorous, self-referential, self-improving problem solvers. It relies on formal proofs to justify code rewrites, ensuring that any self-modification is provably beneficial. However, in practice and without restrictive assumptions about the system, it is impossible to formally prove whether a modification to an AI system will be beneficial. For example, while it may seem that an LLM-based coding agent would benefit from access to more tools (e.g., code search, test runners), the actual impact depends heavily on the model’s training and task context (e.g., a testing tool that is optimized for one setup may confuse the agent when working with others). Instead of requiring formal proofs, we empirically validate self-modifications against a benchmark, allowing the system to improve and explore based on observed results. This approach mirrors biological evolution, where mutations and adaptations are not verified in advance but are produced, trialed, and then selected via natural selection. We also take inspiration from Darwinian evolution (Darwin, 2023) and investigate the effectiveness of maintaining a library of previously discovered agents to serve as stepping stones for future generations. We propose the Darwin Gödel Machine (DGM), a self-referential, self-improving system that writes and modifies its own code to become a better coding agent. Each self-modification requires the DGM to edit its own codebase. We use Python, which is Turing-complete, giving the DGM the potential to build any computable machine. Our framework envisions agents that can rewrite their own training scripts (including training a new foundation model (FM)). However, we do not show that in this paper, as training FMs is computationally intensive and would introduce substantial additional complexity, which we leave as future work. Instead, this paper focuses on improving the design of coding agents with frozen pretrained FMs (e.g., tool use, workflows). The DGM alternates between self-modification and evaluation phases. During the self-modification phase, selected coding agents from the archive generate modified versions of themselves. During the evaluation phase, each modified agent is tested on a coding benchmark, estimating the agent’s coding capabilities, and then added to the archive. By improving its own capabilities through this loop, the DGM becomes better at both solving coding tasks and making future self-improvements. A key assumption is that an increase in performance on coding benchmarks indicates better coding capabilities, and hence better ability to self-modify and self-improve. Furthermore, the DGM maintains an archive of generated coding agents, initialized with only one agent, and continuously accumulates all generated variants over time. To support continual self-improvement, the DGM draws inspiration from openendedness research (Wang et al., 2019; Fernando et al., 2024; Faldor et al., 2025), accumulating diverse stepping stones (i.e., interesting yet suboptimal solutions or features that may enable future breakthroughs). This open-ended exploration encourages the discovery of novel and potentially useful self-modifications beyond immediate performance gains. We present results on two coding benchmarks: SWE-bench (Jimenez et al., 2024) and Polyglot (Paul Gauthier, 2024). The DGM automatically improves itself from 20.0% to 50.0% on SWE-bench, and from 14.2% to 30.7% on Polyglot. We show that self-improvement enables continued progress, as the DGM outperforms the baseline where the same base agent is repeatedly used to modify and

探讨的是：能否安全地自动化对「越来越好的 AI」的搜索。可以想象这样一个系统：它像科学发现本身一样，成为自身进步的引擎——立足既往、递归改进，把自己推向更强的能力。

父代

图 1：Darwin Gödel Machine。DGM 通过交错自修改与下游任务评估，迭代地构建不断增长的智能体档案。档案中的智能体经开放式探索被选中做自修改。

Schmidhuber（2007）提出一类数学上严格的、自指的自改进问题求解器。它依赖形式证明来为代码改写辩护，确保任何自修改都可证有益。然而在实践中，若不施加关于系统的限制性假设，就无法形式证明对 AI 系统的某次修改是否有益。例如，虽然看起来基于 LLM 的编码智能体应能从更多工具（代码搜索、测试运行器等）中获益，实际影响却高度依赖于模型训练与任务语境（为某一套环境优化的测试工具，换到另一套上可能反而让智能体困惑）。我们不再要求形式证明，而是对照基准对自修改做经验验证，让系统依据观测结果改进与探索。这镜像了生物演化：突变与适应事先并不被核验，而是被产生、试错，再经自然选择保留。我们也受达尔文演化（Darwin, 2023）启发，考察维护一座「先前发现的智能体库」、作为后代踏脚石是否有效。

我们提出 Darwin Gödel Machine（DGM）：一种自指、自改进的系统，通过编写并修改自己的代码，成为更好的编码智能体。每一次自修改都要求 DGM 编辑自己的代码库。我们使用图灵完备的 Python，使 DGM 原则上可以构建任何可计算机器。我们的框架设想智能体可以改写自己的训练脚本（包括训练新的基础模型）。但本文并不展示这一点：训练基础模型计算代价高、会引入大量额外复杂度，留作未来工作。本文聚焦于用冻结的预训练基础模型改进编码智能体的设计（例如工具使用、工作流）。DGM 在自修改与评估两阶段之间交替。自修改阶段，从档案中选出的编码智能体生成自身的修改版本；评估阶段，每个修改后的智能体在编码基准上测试，以估计其编码能力，然后加入档案。通过这一循环提升自身能力，DGM 既更擅长求解编码任务，也更擅长做后续自改进。一个关键假设是：编码基准上的性能提升，意味着更好的编码能力，从而意味着更好的自修改与自改进能力。此外，DGM 维护一份已生成编码智能体的档案，初始化时只有一个智能体，并随时间不断累积全部变体。为支持持续自改进，DGM 从开放式研究（Wang et al., 2019; Fernando et al., 2024; Faldor et al., 2025）汲取灵感，积累多样的踏脚石（即有趣但未必最优、却可能催生未来突破的解或特征）。这种开放式探索鼓励发现新颖且潜在有用的自修改，而不只盯眼前的性能增益。

我们在两个编码基准上报告结果：SWE-bench（Jimenez et al., 2024）与 Polyglot（Paul Gauthier, 2024）。DGM 在 SWE-bench 上自动将自身从 20.0% 提升到 50.0%，在 Polyglot 上从 14.2% 提升到 30.7%。我们表明自改进能支撑持续进步：DGM 优于「始终用同一个基座智能体去修改并生成新智能体、自身并不自改进」的基线。

白话解读

本页用图 1 把循环钉死：从档案里挑父代，让它改自己的仓库，再拿基准分数决定要不要进档。理论哥德尔机要形式证明；DGM 改成经验验证，并承认「基准涨分 ≈ 更会改自己」是关键假设。基础模型权重冻住，只改工具与工作流。开放式档案专门存那些当下不最优、日后可能当踏脚石的版本。类比：不是只留冠军苗，而是把整座苗圃都留下来继续杂交。

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 generate new agents without self-improvement. We also show that open-ended exploration and keeping an archive of all previously generated agents lead to the discovery of better coding agents. The DGM outperforms the baseline of not having open-ended exploration (i.e., a baseline without the accumulation of an archive of interestingly different stepping stones), where the coding agent always builds off the most recent version of itself. Overall, the DGM represents a step toward AI systems that can build upon their own prior innovations and improve recursively. We consider and discuss safety aspects extensively, including sandboxing and traceability of self-modifications, to ensure responsible experimentation (Section 5). By advancing the possibility of safe, self-referential, selfimproving models, the DGM moves us closer to AI that not only learns but evolves in an open-ended, self-accelerating trajectory, much like science itself. RELATED WORK Open-Endedness. A grand challenge for driving unbounded innovation is designing open-ended AI systems that continuously generate novel and learnable artifacts (Stanley et al., 2017). Hughes et al. (2024) characterized open-endedness as a system’s capacity to generate sequences of artifacts that are both novel and learnable from an observer’s perspective. A central difficulty lies in structuring and exploring vast search spaces to consistently produce artifacts that are interesting to humans (Clune, 2019; Jiang et al., 2023). Early progress drew on quality-diversity algorithms, goal-directed exploration, intrinsic motivation, and learning-progress frameworks (Pugh et al., 2016; Ecoffet et al., 2019; Lehman & Stanley, 2011; Oudeyer et al., 2007), while recent advances leverage large-scale foundation models (FMs) as proxies for human interestingness and versatile engines for generating and evaluating novel behaviors across diverse domains (Brown et al., 2020; Hu et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2024b). However, these approaches have yet to close the self-referential self-improvement loop, meaning improvements on downstream tasks do not translate into enhanced capabilities for self-modification or the acceleration of further innovations. We aim to mimic the acceleration of science and technology, where new tools and discoveries catalyze the creation of even more discoveries. How can we emulate nature’s arc of evolution, which bends not only toward complexity but also an ever greater capacity to evolve (Dawkins, 2019; Gerhart & Kirschner, 2007; Hendrikse et al., 2007)? Meta-Learning FM Agents. Many FM-based agents are handcrafted. Some building blocks include prompt engineering (Chen et al., 2023; Schulhoff et al., 2024), chain-of-thought (Wei et al., 2022; Yao et al., 2023; Hu & Clune, 2024; Guo et al., 2025; Lightman et al., 2023; Muennighoff et al., 2025; Zelikman et al., 2024a), self-reflection (Shinn et al., 2023; Yao et al., 2023; Madaan et al., 2023), multi-agent debate (Zhuge et al., 2023; Liang et al., 2023; Khan et al., 2024), memory (Liu et al., 2023; Zhong et al., 2024; Modarressi et al., 2023), temperature sampling (Zhu et al., 2024), and retrieval augmented generation (Lewis et al., 2020). The manual composition of these components limits the system’s abilities to the ingenuity of its human designer. More recently, several meta-learning approaches have emerged that leverage FM to automatically optimize prompts (Fernando et al., 2024; , FAIR; Khattab et al., 2023; Cheng et al., 2024; Yuksekgonul et al., 2024; Yuan et al., 2024) and design agentic modules (Zhang et al., 2024c; Zhou et al., 2024; Yin et al., 2024; Zhuge et al., 2024; Rosser & Foerster, 2025; Zhang et al., 2025a; Ye et al., 2025; Gao et al., 2025; Nie et al., 2025; Su et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025b; Niu et al., 2025). The Automated Design of Agentic Systems (ADAS, Hu et al., 2025) iteratively generates downstream agents with a fixed meta-agent, evaluates them against a target benchmark, and incorporates feedback to refine subsequent generations. In contrast, the DGM is a single system that both solves downstream tasks (i.e., coding problems) and refines its own implementation (i.e., its codebase), removing the need for a fixed, handcrafted meta-agent and enabling self-referential improvements. Self-Improving AI. Early on, various researchers outlined theoretical and conceptual approaches to self-improvement (Good, 1966; Schmidhuber, 1987; 2007). Some practical approaches to automated self-improvement include systems defined by neural network weight parameterizations (Schmidhuber, 1993; Hall, 2007; Hobbhahn, 2025; Kirsch & Schmidhuber, 2022; Irie et al., 2022; 2025; Lu et al., 2023; Havrilla et al., 2024b). Metz et al. (2021) developed a gradient-based optimizer that is selfreferentially meta-trained using a variant of population-based training (Jaderberg et al., 2017). Lange et al. (2023) extended this approach to gradient-free learning. Silver et al. (2017) used self-play to continuously evolve agents, achieving superhuman performance in challenging domains such as chess and Go. More closely related to the DGM are recent approaches that leverage FM-based agents for self-improvement (Yin et al., 2024; Robeyns et al., 2025; Hu et al., 2024; Zelikman

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

在没有自改进的情况下生成新智能体。我们也表明，开放式探索并保留所有先前生成智能体的档案，能导向更好编码智能体的发现。DGM 优于「没有开放式探索」（即不累积有趣而不同的踏脚石档案）的基线——那种设定下，编码智能体总是从自己的最近一版往下改。总体而言，DGM 朝「能立足自身先前创新、递归改进」的 AI 系统迈进了一步。我们广泛考虑并讨论安全问题，包括沙箱与自修改的可追溯性，以确保负责任的实验（第 5 节）。通过推进安全、自指、自改进模型的可能性，DGM 使我们更接近不仅会学习、而且会以开放式、自我加速轨迹演化的 AI——很像科学本身。

相关工作

开放式。驱动无界创新的一大挑战，是设计能持续生成新颖且可学习产物的开放式 AI 系统（Stanley et al., 2017）。Hughes et al.（2024）把开放式刻画为：从观察者视角看，系统能生成既新颖又可学习的产物序列。核心困难在于如何结构化并探索巨大搜索空间，以持续产出对人类有趣的产物（Clune, 2019; Jiang et al., 2023）。早期进展来自质量–多样性算法、目标导向探索、内在动机与学习进度框架（Pugh et al., 2016; Ecoffet et al., 2019; Lehman & Stanley, 2011; Oudeyer et al., 2007）；近期则借助大规模基础模型作为「人类有趣性」的代理，以及跨领域生成与评估新行为的多功能引擎（Brown et al., 2020; Hu et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2024b）。然而这些方法尚未闭合自指的自改进环：下游任务上的改进，并不会转化成更强的自修改能力，也不会加速后续创新。我们想模仿科学与技术的加速：新工具与新发现催化更多发现。如何效仿自然演化那条弧线——它不仅弯向复杂性，也弯向越来越强的「演化能力」本身（Dawkins, 2019; Gerhart & Kirschner, 2007; Hendrikse et al., 2007）？

元学习基础模型智能体。许多基于基础模型的智能体是人手打造的。构件包括提示工程（Chen et al., 2023; Schulhoff et al., 2024）、思维链（Wei et al., 2022; Yao et al., 2023; Hu & Clune, 2024; Guo et al., 2025; Lightman et al., 2023; Muennighoff et al., 2025; Zelikman et al., 2024a）、自我反思（Shinn et al., 2023; Yao et al., 2023; Madaan et al., 2023）、远程智能体辩论（Zhuge et al., 2023; Liang et al., 2023; Khan et al., 2024）、记忆（Liu et al., 2023; Zhong et al., 2024; Modarressi et al., 2023）、温度采样（Zhu et al., 2024）以及检索增强生成（Lewis et al., 2020）。这些构件的手工组合，把系统能力锁在人类设计者的巧思之内。近来出现若干元学习方法，用基础模型自动优化提示（Fernando et al., 2024; FAIR; Khattab et al., 2023; Cheng et al., 2024; Yuksekgonul et al., 2024; Yuan et al., 2024）并设计智能体模块（Zhang et al., 2024c; Zhou et al., 2024; Yin et al., 2024; Zhuge et al., 2024; Rosser & Foerster, 2025; Zhang et al., 2025a; Ye et al., 2025; Gao et al., 2025; Nie et al., 2025; Su et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2025b; Niu et al., 2025）。Automated Design of Agentic Systems（ADAS, Hu et al., 2025）用固定的元智能体迭代生成下游智能体，对照目标基准评估，再把反馈纳入后续世代。相比之下，DGM 是单一系统：既求解下游任务（编码问题），也精炼自己的实现（自己的代码库），从而去掉固定、人手打造的元智能体，使自指改进成为可能。

自改进 AI。早期已有研究者勾勒自改进的理论与概念路径（Good, 1966; Schmidhuber, 1987; 2007）。若干实践路径把系统参数化为神经网络权重（Schmidhuber, 1993; Hall, 2007; Hobbhahn, 2025; Kirsch & Schmidhuber, 2022; Irie et al., 2022; 2025; Lu et al., 2023; Havrilla et al., 2024b）。Metz et al.（2021）开发了一种基于梯度的优化器，用群体训练的变体做自指元训练（Jaderberg et al., 2017）。Lange et al.（2023）把该方法扩展到无梯度学习。Silver et al.（2017）用自对弈持续演化智能体，在国际象棋与围棋等困难领域达到超人类水平。与 DGM 更接近的是近期用基于基础模型的智能体做自改进的工作（Yin et al., 2024; Robeyns et al., 2025; Hu et al., 2024; Zelikman

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 et al., 2024b; Huang et al., 2022; Singh et al., 2023). Zelikman et al. (2024b) use a meta-agent to generate downstream agents, updating the meta-agent based on the meta-utility derived from the generated solutions. Yin et al. (2024) use a single system to both solve downstream tasks and recursively modify itself. However, the downstream tasks or the meta-utility do not always align with the capabilities required for self-improvement. In the DGM, improvement in downstream tasks directly reflects an increase in self-improvement ability, enabling the potential for self-accelerating progress. Most similar is concurrent work by Robeyns et al. (2025), which also has a single agent recursively solving coding problems and modifying its own codebase. The main difference from Robeyns et al. (2025) (and also Zelikman et al. (2024b); Yin et al. (2024)) is that the DGM has an open-ended exploration loop, encouraging self-modifications beyond immediate performance gains and thus avoiding stagnation in suboptimal states. Appendix B also discusses additional related work on program synthesis and Darwinian evolution. DARWIN GÖDEL MACHINE A Gödel Machine is a theoretical idea of an AI that searches for ways that provably improve itself (Schmidhuber, 2007). In this paper, we propose Darwin Gödel Machine (DGM), an attempt to realize the long-held dream of creating a Gödel Machine. The DGM relaxes the Gödel Machine’s impractical requirement of theoretically proving that a change will improve the system, instead requiring empirical evidence from experiments to demonstrate that a proposed new version enhances performance. Additionally, since the DGM relies on empirical evidence of improvement, it may get stuck in a local optimum within the vast search space of possible systems (i.e., all computable algorithms). To address this, the DGM maintains an archive of discovered solutions during the search, facilitating open-ended exploration rather than relying on evolving a single solution. Since the principles echo Darwinian evolution (Darwin, 2023) (Appendix B), where new innovations emerge by selecting an entity from an archive of previously discovered solutions, modifying it, and keeping it if it is interestingly new (Zhang et al., 2024b; Faldor et al., 2025; Stanley & Lehman, 2015), we call our algorithm a Darwin Gödel Machine (Figure 1). Self-referential Self-improvement of Coding Agents. The DGM is initialized with only one coding agent, and its progression is evaluated on coding benchmarks. A coding agent is defined as a single system, implemented with a code repository and powered by frozen pretrained foundation models (FMs), capable of reading, writing, and executing code. Code, when expressed in a general-purpose Turing-complete language (e.g., Python), is a powerful medium for building and improving intelligent systems because it can represent any computable process. Recent works (Hu et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2024c) demonstrate that such agents can be improved through meta-learning of their designs (e.g., prompts, workflows, and tools), which are implemented in their code repository. Therefore, we define self-improvement as a coding task that involves modifying the design of an agent’s own components (i.e., its own code, which does not include the open-ended exploration process described in the next paragraph). The key motivation is that the empirical evidence must reflect the system’s ability to both self-improve and solve downstream tasks. By configuring the DGM as a coding agent and testing its coding capabilities, the observed improvements demonstrate not only enhanced performance in downstream tasks but also the capacity for further self-improvement, as self-improvement is fundamentally a coding task that modifies the coding agent’s own code repository. Population-based Open-ended Exploration. Starting from a single initial coding agent, the DGM builds an archive of all discovered agents. In each iteration, the DGM selects parent agents to selfmodify and branch off to produce new agents. Parent selection is roughly proportional to each agent’s performance score and inversely proportional to the number of its children with codebase-editing functionality (Appendix C.2). This favors high-performing agents that have been underexplored (i.e., have fewer existing children), thereby promoting both exploitation of strong performers and exploration of promising but less-sampled lineages. All agents retain a non-zero selection probability, ensuring that any path to improvement remains feasible given sufficient compute. Each selected parent analyzes its own benchmark evaluation logs, proposes the next feature to implement, and receives this proposal as a problem statement to execute (Appendix C.3). The parent then implements the suggested feature into its own codebase, generating a new coding agent. Each newly generated agent is quantitatively evaluated on a chosen coding benchmark to estimate its coding abilities. Only agents that compile successfully and retain the ability to edit a given codebase are added to the DGM archive, as only they can continue self-modification. All others are discarded. The cycle of

Darwin Gödel Machine

哥德尔机是一种理论构想：AI 搜索那些可证能改进自身的改法（Schmidhuber, 2007）。本文提出 Darwin Gödel Machine（DGM），试图实现这一长久梦想。DGM 放松了哥德尔机「必须从理论上证明改动会改进系统」这一不切实际的要求，改为要求来自实验的经验证据，表明所提议的新版本提升了性能。此外，正因为 DGM 依赖改进的经验证据，它可能在可能系统（即全部可计算算法）的巨大搜索空间中陷入局部最优。为此，DGM 在搜索过程中维护一份已发现解的档案，以促进开放式探索，而不是只演化单一解。这些原则呼应达尔文演化（Darwin, 2023）（附录 B）：从先前发现的解的档案中选出一个实体、修改它，若它有趣地新就保留（Zhang et al., 2024b; Faldor et al., 2025; Stanley & Lehman, 2015）。因此我们称该算法为 Darwin Gödel Machine（图 1）。

编码智能体的自指自改进。DGM 初始化时只有一个编码智能体，其进展在编码基准上评估。编码智能体定义为单一系统：以代码仓库实现、由冻结的预训练基础模型驱动，能够读、写并执行代码。用通用图灵完备语言（例如 Python）表达的代码，是构建与改进智能系统的有力媒介，因为它可以表示任何可计算过程。近期工作（Hu et al., 2025; Zhang et al., 2024c）表明，这类智能体可通过对其设计（提示、工作流、工具）做元学习而改进，而这些设计就写在它们的代码仓库里。因此，我们把自改进定义为一项编码任务：修改智能体自身构件的设计（即它自己的代码；下一段描述的开放式探索过程不在此列）。关键动机是：经验证据必须同时反映系统自改进与求解下游任务的能力。把 DGM 配成编码智能体并测试其编码能力，观测到的改进就不仅是下游性能提升，也是进一步自改进的能力——因为自改进本质上就是修改编码智能体自己代码仓库的编码任务。

基于群体的开放式探索。从单一初始编码智能体出发，DGM 构建一份包含所有已发现智能体的档案。每一轮迭代，DGM 选择父代智能体做自修改并分枝，产生新智能体。父代选择大致与各智能体的性能分数成正比，并与其具有代码库编辑功能的子代数成反比（附录 C.2）。这偏向高表现、但尚未被充分探索（子代较少）的智能体，从而同时利用强者并探索有前景、采样较少的谱系。所有智能体都保留非零被选概率，确保只要算力足够，任何改进路径都仍可行。每个被选父代分析自己的基准评估日志，提议下一步要实现的特征，并把该提议当作问题陈述来执行（附录 C.3）。父代随即把建议特征实现进自己的代码库，生成新的编码智能体。每个新生成的智能体在选定编码基准上定量评估，以估计其编码能力。只有成功编译、并仍保有编辑给定代码库能力的智能体才会加入 DGM 档案，因为只有它们能继续自修改。其余一律丢弃。这一循环

白话解读

本页把方法钉成两刀。第一刀：自改进本身被定义成「改自己仓库」的编码任务，所以基准涨分被当成更会改自己的证据。第二刀：档案里谁当父代，大致看分数高、子代少，人人都留一点被抽中的机会。和同期「只改最近一版」的工作比，开放式环是为了不卡死。类比：不是只培养现任冠军，而是整座族谱都能再分枝。

第 5 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 parent selection, self-modification, and evaluation continues, progressively growing the archive of solutions. Importantly, we note that archived solutions can serve as stepping stones that result in improvements much later than their original discovery, making our approach substantially different from hill-climbing agentic design approaches (Robeyns et al., 2025). Currently, the open-ended exploration process (i.e., archive maintenance, parent selection) is fixed and not modifiable by the DGM, which we leave as an avenue for future work. Appendix C.4 shows the pseudocode for the DGM algorithm. EXPERIMENTS Section 4.1 describes the experimental setup, including the initial coding agent that initializes the DGM. We conduct experiments on two coding benchmarks: SWE-bench (Jimenez et al., 2024) and Polyglot (Paul Gauthier, 2024) (Section 4.2). For each benchmark, we compare the DGM against two baselines: DGM without self-improving agents and DGM without open-ended exploration (Section 4.3). Across all experiments, we find that the DGM outperforms both baselines, showing that the self-improvement mechanism and open-ended exploration are essential for sustained performance gains (Section 4.4). Furthermore, we show that the features discovered by the DGM transfer across models, benchmarks, and tasks (Section 4.4). 4.1 EXPERIMENT SETUP The DGM is initialized with a single coding agent. This base agent is built around a frozen FM and augmented with tool use capabilities (Schick et al., 2023; Anthropic, 2024b). The FM autonomously determines its action at each step, using whichever tool it deems appropriate. The base agent has access to two tools: a Bash tool for executing bash commands, and an edit tool for viewing and editing directories and entire files (Appendix C.1). The agent receives a single input prompt that specifies the location of the target repository, the problem statement, and the repository’s testing framework (Appendix C.1). The base agent is intentionally lightweight to provide a simple starting point from which self-improvement can be clearly demonstrated. We run the DGM for 80 iterations (generating one new agent per iteration), with two iterations running in parallel for SWE-bench and four for Polyglot (Appendix C.2). During self-modifications, coding agents are powered by Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) (Anthropic, 2024a) in both SWE-bench and Polyglot experiments. During benchmark evaluation, coding agents are powered by Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) for SWE-bench and o3-mini (OpenAI, 2025) for Polyglot (Appendix D.1). 4.2 BENCHMARKS We evaluate the DGM on two popular benchmarks that assess different aspects of coding tasks to validate the algorithm’s effectiveness across various use cases. Both SWE-bench and Polyglot are benchmarks that evaluate how well AI agents solve coding tasks automatically. Given a code repository and a task instruction, the agent is expected to make changes to the repository in order to fulfill the task. Both SWE-bench and Polyglot are widely used benchmarks (Zhang et al., 2024d;a; Xia et al., 2024; Cao et al., 2024; Google DeepMind, 2025; Gauthier, 2024) that require the AI agent to navigate a code repository, understand the interplay between functions in different files, and spot small errors in convoluted code. SWE-bench only has Python tasks, while Polyglot has tasks in multiple programming languages. Another difference is that each SWE-bench task may require edits to multiple files, whereas each Polyglot task primarily involves implementing a solution from scratch in a single file (although the agent still needs to examine other files to understand what changes are necessary), resulting in fewer file edits overall. SWE-bench. To avoid wasting compute on unsolvable tasks, we use SWE-bench Verified (OpenAI, 2024), a human-filtered subset of SWE-bench (Jimenez et al., 2024) where all tasks are solvable. Throughout this paper, the term SWE-bench refers by default to to the SWE-bench Verified subset. Polyglot. Polyglot includes tasks in multiple programming languages (C++, Rust, Python, etc.) (Paul Gauthier, 2024). Compared to SWE-bench, one of the most widely used coding benchmarks and likely included in the training sets of FMs, Polyglot is more niche and less likely to be included in FMs’ post-training data. Additionally, Polyglot is primarily used by its developer to evaluate Aider (Gauthier, 2024). This provides an opportunity to compare automatically designed agents with

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

父代选择、自修改与评估持续进行，档案中的解逐步增多。重要的是，已归档的解可以充当踏脚石，其带来的改进可能远晚于最初被发现的时刻，这使我们的方法与爬山式智能体设计（Robeyns et al., 2025）有本质不同。目前，开放式探索过程（即档案维护、父代选择）是固定的，DGM 不能修改它，我们将其留作未来工作。附录 C.4 给出 DGM 算法的伪代码。

实验

第 4.1 节描述实验设置，包括初始化 DGM 的初始编码智能体。我们在两个编码基准上做实验：SWE-bench（Jimenez et al., 2024）与 Polyglot（Paul Gauthier, 2024）（第 4.2 节）。对每个基准，我们将 DGM 与两条基线对比：没有自改进智能体的 DGM，以及没有开放式探索的 DGM（第 4.3 节）。在全部实验中，DGM 都优于这两条基线，表明自改进机制与开放式探索对持续性能增益都必不可少（第 4.4 节）。此外，我们表明 DGM 发现的特征可以在模型、基准与任务之间迁移（第 4.4 节）。

4.1 实验设置

DGM 由单一编码智能体初始化。该基座智能体围绕冻结的基础模型构建，并增强以工具使用能力（Schick et al., 2023; Anthropic, 2024b）。基础模型在每一步自主决定动作，使用它认为合适的工具。基座智能体有两个工具：用于执行 bash 命令的 Bash 工具，以及用于查看与编辑目录和整文件的编辑工具（附录 C.1）。智能体收到一条输入提示，指明目标仓库位置、问题陈述以及仓库的测试框架（附录 C.1）。基座智能体有意保持轻量，以便从一个简单起点清晰展示自改进。我们运行 DGM 共 80 次迭代（每轮生成一个新智能体），SWE-bench 上两轮并行，Polyglot 上四轮并行（附录 C.2）。自修改时，编码智能体由 Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New)（Anthropic, 2024a）驱动，SWE-bench 与 Polyglot 实验皆然。基准评估时，SWE-bench 上由 Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) 驱动，Polyglot 上由 o3-mini（OpenAI, 2025）驱动（附录 D.1）。

4.2 基准

我们在两个流行基准上评估 DGM，它们考查编码任务的不同侧面，以验证算法在多种用例下的有效性。SWE-bench 与 Polyglot 都评估 AI 智能体自动求解编码任务的能力。给定代码仓库与任务指令，智能体应修改仓库以完成任务。二者都是广泛使用的基准（Zhang et al., 2024d;a; Xia et al., 2024; Cao et al., 2024; Google DeepMind, 2025; Gauthier, 2024），要求智能体在仓库中导航、理解不同文件中函数的相互作用，并在缠绕的代码里找出小错误。SWE-bench 只有 Python 任务，Polyglot 则覆盖多种编程语言。另一差异是：每道 SWE-bench 任务可能要改多个文件，而每道 Polyglot 任务主要是在单文件里从零实现解（尽管智能体仍需查看其他文件以理解必须改什么），因此总体文件编辑更少。

SWE-bench。为避免把算力浪费在不可解任务上，我们使用 SWE-bench Verified（OpenAI, 2024），即经人工筛选、全部可解的 SWE-bench 子集（Jimenez et al., 2024）。本文中，SWE-bench 默认即指 SWE-bench Verified 子集。

Polyglot。Polyglot 包含多种编程语言（C++、Rust、Python 等）的任务（Paul Gauthier, 2024）。相对 SWE-bench——目前最常用的编码基准之一，很可能已进入基础模型的训练集——Polyglot 更小众，更不可能进入基础模型的后训练数据。此外，Polyglot 主要由其开发者用来评估 Aider（Gauthier, 2024）。这提供了把自动设计的智能体与

白话解读

本页交代实验怎么跑。档案可以当很晚才兑现的踏脚石，所以不是只朝当前最优爬山。基座智能体故意很瘦：Bash 加整文件编辑，基础模型冻住。跑八十轮；SWE-bench 用 Claude 3.5 又改又评，Polyglot 用 Claude 改、o3-mini 评。SWE-bench 是多文件 Python 修 issue；Polyglot 是多语言、偏单文件从零写。类比：先给一把钝扳手，看它能不能自己磨出细锉。

第 6 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 a representative coding agent in the community, which has been continuously developed and tested against the Polyglot benchmark over a year by human developers. Finally, Polyglot enables testing of the DGM’s ability to generalize across multiple programming languages beyond just Python. We follow the Polyglot leaderboard setup (Paul Gauthier, 2024), with one key difference: the leaderboard reports pass@2, where the agent can view feedback from ground-truth tests once, whereas we report pass@1, where the agent never sees ground-truth test results. Since it is expensive to evaluate on the full benchmarks (Appendix E.1), we use a staged evaluation strategy to estimate the coding agent’s performance. In general, we evaluate the coding agent on more tasks when we are more confident that it is a relatively strong performer. We first evaluate each coding agent on a set of 10 tasks to verify basic codebase-editing functionality. Only agents that retain the ability to edit code can solve downstream coding tasks or perform further selfmodifications. Agents that pass this initial stage are then evaluated on an expanded set of 50 tasks. For SWE-bench, tasks are selected based on SWE-bench-verified-mini (Hobbhahn, 2025), which is designed to be a representative sample of the full benchmark (Appendix E.2). For Polyglot, tasks are chosen as a random subset of the full benchmark (Appendix E.3). Because the LLMs we use are inherently stochastic, performance can be noisy. We use smaller subset evaluations to estimate general effectiveness and identify top candidates. For SWE-bench, if a coding agent achieves over 40% success (a heuristic chosen based on the noise observed in preliminary runs) on the 60-task subset and ranks among the top two performers in the archive, it is then evaluated on 200 tasks (140 plus the previous 60) to more accurately assess its true coding capability (Appendix E.2). For Polyglot, if a coding agent achieves over 40% success on the 10-task subset, it is evaluated on an expanded 50-task subset (Appendix E.3). 4.3 BASELINES To isolate and evaluate the contribution of the DGM’s core components, we compare the DGM against two baselines: DGM without self-improving agents (DGM w/o self-improve) and DGM without open-ended exploration (DGM w/o open-ended exploration). DGM w/o self-improve replicates the approach of ADAS (Hu et al., 2025) in this setting, wherein the meta agent responsible for modifying the coding agents remains fixed as the base agent throughout the experiment (Algorithm 2). This baseline allows us to assess the role of iterative self-improvement in accelerating the evolution of better coding agents. DGM w/o open-ended exploration eliminates the use of an archive and always self-modifies the latest stored version of itself (Algorithm 3). If a coding agent self-modifies to the point where it loses the basic functionality required to edit a codebase, it can no longer modify itself or solve any coding task. Therefore, DGM w/o open-ended exploration retains the latest version of itself that still maintains the basic functionality for codebase editing. This baseline allows us to evaluate the impact of having an archive and the well-documented beneficial principles of open-ended exploration (Clune, 2019; Stanley & Lehman, 2015; Zhang et al., 2024b; Fernando et al., 2024; Lee et al., 2020; Samvelyan et al., 2024; Colas et al., 2022b) in guiding the agent’s evolution. In addition to the learned baselines, we compare the DGM against handcrafted, open-source solutions. For SWE-bench, we take the state-of-the-art (SoTA) open-source solution that has been checked (i.e., the SWE-bench team was able to reproduce the results) (Appendix E.4). For Polyglot, we take the representative agent (Aider) (Gauthier, 2024), which is open-sourced and designed to support multiple programming languages and large codebase editing (Appendix E.5). For a fair comparison, we measure the percentage of solved tasks on the same benchmark subsets used to evaluate the DGM (Appendix E.2, Appendix E.3). These values are shown as dotted horizontal lines in Figure 2. 4.4 RESULTS After 80 iterations of the DGM, the coding agent’s performance increases from 20.0% to 50.0% on SWE-bench, and from 14.0% to 38.0% on Polyglot (Figure 2). Since the DGM is evaluated on only 50 tasks in the Polyglot experiment setup (Section 4.2), we additionally evaluate both the base agent and the best DGM-discovered agent on the full Polyglot benchmark to more accurately estimate the improvement. On the full Polyglot benchmark, the DGM improves the coding agent from 14.2% to 30.7%. This shows that the DGM can automatically self-improve to create a better coding agent. Moreover, the performance of the best DGM-discovered agent is comparable to that of the checked, open-source, human-designed SoTA on SWE-bench (Figure 2). On Polyglot, although the DGM

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

社区中有代表性的编码智能体对比：后者由人类开发者对照 Polyglot 基准持续开发、测试逾一年。最后，Polyglot 还能检验 DGM 在 Python 之外多种编程语言上的泛化。我们遵循 Polyglot 排行榜设置（Paul Gauthier, 2024），有一处关键不同：排行榜报告 pass@2（智能体可看一次来自标准答案测试的反馈），我们报告 pass@1（智能体从未看到标准答案测试结果）。

由于在完整基准上评估很昂贵（附录 E.1），我们采用分阶段评估策略来估计编码智能体的性能。总体原则是：越有把握它是相对强者，就越在更多任务上评估。我们先在 10 道任务上评估每个编码智能体，以核验基本的代码库编辑功能。只有仍能编辑代码的智能体，才能求解下游编码任务或做进一步自修改。通过这一初筛的智能体，再在扩展的 50 道任务上评估。对 SWE-bench，任务按 SWE-bench-verified-mini（Hobbhahn, 2025）选取，该子集被设计为完整基准的代表性样本（附录 E.2）。对 Polyglot，任务是完整基准的随机子集（附录 E.3）。由于所用 LLM 固有随机性，性能会有噪声。我们用较小子集估计总体效力并识别顶尖候选。对 SWE-bench，若编码智能体在 60 题子集上成功率超过 40%（该启发式基于预实验中的噪声选定）且在档案中位列前两名，则再在 200 题（前 60 加另外 140）上评估，以更准确地衡量其真实编码能力（附录 E.2）。对 Polyglot，若编码智能体在 10 题子集上成功率超过 40%，则再在扩展的 50 题子集上评估（附录 E.3）。

4.3 基线

为隔离并评估 DGM 核心构件的贡献，我们将 DGM 与两条基线对比：没有自改进智能体的 DGM（DGM w/o self-improve）以及没有开放式探索的 DGM（DGM w/o open-ended exploration）。DGM w/o self-improve 在本设定中复现 ADAS（Hu et al., 2025）：负责修改编码智能体的元智能体在整个实验中固定为基座智能体（算法 2）。该基线让我们评估迭代自改进在加速更好编码智能体演化中的作用。DGM w/o open-ended exploration 取消档案，总是自修改自己最近保存的版本（算法 3）。若编码智能体自修改到丧失编辑代码库所需的基本功能，它就再也不能修改自己或求解任何编码任务。因此该基线保留仍具备代码库编辑基本功能的最近一版。该基线让我们评估「拥有档案」以及开放式探索那些被充分记录的有益原则（Clune, 2019; Stanley & Lehman, 2015; Zhang et al., 2024b; Fernando et al., 2024; Lee et al., 2020; Samvelyan et al., 2024; Colas et al., 2022b）对引导智能体演化的影响。

除学习得到的基线外，我们还将 DGM 与人手打造的开源方案对比。对 SWE-bench，我们取经核验的开源 SoTA（即 SWE-bench 团队能复现结果）（附录 E.4）。对 Polyglot，我们取有代表性的智能体 Aider（Gauthier, 2024），它开源、支持多语言与大型代码库编辑（附录 E.5）。为公平比较，我们在评估 DGM 所用的同一基准子集上测量已解决任务百分比（附录 E.2、附录 E.3）。这些数值在图 2 中以虚水平线标出。

4.4 结果

DGM 经过 80 次迭代后，编码智能体在 SWE-bench 上的性能从 20.0% 升至 50.0%，在 Polyglot 上从 14.0% 升至 38.0%（图 2）。由于 Polyglot 实验设置只在 50 题上评估（第 4.2 节），我们额外在完整 Polyglot 基准上评估基座智能体与 DGM 发现的最佳智能体，以更准确估计提升。在完整 Polyglot 上，DGM 将编码智能体从 14.2% 提升到 30.7%。这表明 DGM 能自动自改进以造出更好的编码智能体。而且，DGM 发现的最佳智能体与经核验的开源、人手设计 SoTA 在 SWE-bench 上相当（图 2）。在 Polyglot 上，尽管 DGM

图 3：DGM 自动自改进，成为更好的编码智能体。（左）SWE-bench 上 DGM 运行期间生成的编码智能体档案。每个节点是一个编码智能体，节点 0 对应基座智能体。节点颜色表示 SWE-bench 性能（已解决问题百分比），边框颜色反映该智能体被评估的任务数。边表示哪些智能体自修改产生了子代。许多通往创新的路径会穿过表现较低的节点；关键创新（如节点 24）会引爆建立其上的一波创新。这两点都凸显开放式搜索的益处。（右）DGM 在 SWE-bench 上的进展图。浅蓝线是所有具备基本代码库编辑功能的智能体的平均分。蓝线跟踪档案中任一智能体在各迭代达到的最佳分数。深色线是最终最佳已发现智能体及其前驱节点的谱系，其中包含两次性能回落。这说明开放式搜索的好处：探索多样、有趣的踏脚石，而不是只从迄今最佳解分枝。

起步时基座智能体的表现低于 Aider，它仍发现了远超 Aider 的智能体（图 2）。DGM 发现的智能体在两个基准上都与人手打造的智能体相当或更优。SWE-bench 的 SoTA 智能体与 Aider 由人类辛苦塑造；DGM 暗示未来这类巧思可以被自动化，经由持续自改进的自指循环演化出来。

Workflow improvements include making multiple attempts to solve a task and using another FM to evaluate and select the best solution. Other workflow improvements include considering previous attempts when generating subsequent ones. Appendix F.1 and Appendix F.2 show all modifications leading up to the final best-discovered agents on SWE-bench and Polyglot respectively. Because open-ended exploration allows branching from any agent in the archive with non-zero probability, the DGM can get out of deceptive dips or peaks in performance. For example, at iterations 4 and 56 of the experiment on SWE-bench, although the agent’s score temporarily fell below that of its parent, the DGM was still able to explore innovations along that path and create a new agent that outperformed all of its predecessors (Figure 3). Furthermore, open-ended exploration allows different implementations of the same target functionality to be attempted. For example, while the goal is to provide finer-grained editing tools, the specific implementation of this feature can vary greatly and hence lead to very different performance (Appendix G). The DGM can explore multiple implementations to find the most suitable one and avoid getting trapped in a suboptimal one. The DGM outperforms the baselines of DGM w/o self-improve and DGM w/o open-ended exploration on both benchmarks (Figure 2). Without updating the meta agent that modifies coding agents, DGM w/o self-improve improves the agents in early iterations, but its gains taper off quickly (Appendix A.1). In DGM w/o open-ended exploration, only the most recent agent is retained, so a poorly performing self-modification makes subsequent improvements harder to achieve (Appendix A.1). o3-mini Claude 3.5 Sonnet Claude 3.7 Sonnet Success Rate on SWE-bench (%) 23.0% 20.0% 19.0% 33.0% 50.0% 59.0% Model Transfer on SWE-bench SWE-bench Polyglot Success Rate (%) 20.0% 14.2% 24.5% 28.9% 50.0% 30.7% Zero-shot Transfer Between Benchmarks python task non-python task Success Rate on Polyglot (%) 8.8% 15.2% 11.8% 17.3% 17.6% 33.0% 20.6% 30.8% Task Transfer on Polyglot Base agent Best agent searched with Claude 3.5 Sonnet Best agent transfer to other FMs Base agent Best agent searched on SWE-bench Best agent searched on Polyglot Base agent Aider DGM search on all language DGM search only on python Figure 4: Transfer between Models, Benchmarks, and Tasks. The superior performance of DGM-discovered agents can be transferred across (Left) different models, (Middle) benchmarks, and (Right) different programming language tasks in Polyglot, such as from Python tasks to C++ tasks. To evaluate the generality of the improvements from the DGM, we tested the base agent (Section 4.1) and the best agent discovered during the DGM run (Figure 2) with different FMs than those used during optimization. For SWE-bench, where the DGM was run using Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New), we replaced the FM with Claude 3.7 Sonnet (Anthropic, 2025) or o3-mini, and evaluated on 200 tasks (Figure 4, Left). With o3-mini, the base agent achieved 23.0% and the DGM-discovered agent 33.0%. With Claude 3.7 Sonnet, the base agent achieved 19.0% and the DGM-discovered agent 59.5%. These results suggest that the DGM yields improvements that generalize across FMs, rather than being tightly coupled to the specific FM used during its run (Figure 4). Model transfer results on Polyglot are presented in Appendix A.2. Furthermore, we investigate the transferability of the DGM-discovered agent across different benchmarks and programming languages. First, we evaluate the best DGM-discovered agent from one benchmark (e.g., SWE-bench) on a completely held-out benchmark (e.g., Polyglot), and vice versa (Figure 4, Middle). The best agent evolved on SWE-bench achieves 28.9% on Polyglot, compared to the initial agent’s baseline of 14.2%. Conversely, the best agent evolved on Polyglot achieves 24.5% on SWE-bench, outperforming the original baseline of 20.0%. Since each agent was optimized without ever accessing the alternate benchmark, these evaluations represent truly held-out tests. The consistent performance gains across benchmarks support our claim that DGM’s improvements reflect general skill acquisition rather than overfitting or exploitation of benchmark-specific artifacts. Second, we experiment with a version of the DGM trained exclusively on Python tasks from Polyglot and then transfer the discovered agent to tasks in other languages. Focusing primarily on Python tasks slightly improves performance on Python tasks but reduces performance on non-Python tasks compared to the DGM trained on all languages (Figure 4, Right). However, after being transferred from Python to other unseen languages during the search, the agent still achieves performance comparable to that

因为开放式探索允许以非零概率从档案中任一智能体分枝，DGM 能走出欺骗性的性能低谷或高峰。例如，在 SWE-bench 实验的第 4 与第 56 次迭代，尽管智能体分数暂时低于其父代，DGM 仍能沿该路径探索创新，并造出优于所有前驱的新智能体（图 3）。此外，开放式探索允许对同一目标功能尝试不同实现。例如，目标都是提供更细粒度的编辑工具，具体实现可以差很多，从而带来很不同的性能（附录 G）。DGM 可以探索多种实现以找到最合适的，并避免困在次优实现里。

图 4：跨模型、基准与任务的迁移。DGM 发现的智能体的优越性能可以迁移到（左）不同模型、（中）基准，以及（右）Polyglot 中不同编程语言任务，例如从 Python 任务到 C++ 任务。

为评估 DGM 改进的一般性，我们用不同于优化期间所用的基础模型，测试基座智能体（第 4.1 节）与 DGM 运行中发现的最佳智能体（图 2）。对 SWE-bench，DGM 用 Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) 运行，我们把基础模型换成 Claude 3.7 Sonnet（Anthropic, 2025）或 o3-mini，并在 200 题上评估（图 4，左）。用 o3-mini，基座 23.0%、DGM 发现的智能体 33.0%。用 Claude 3.7 Sonnet，基座 19.0%、DGM 发现的智能体 59.5%。这些结果表明 DGM 产生的改进能跨基础模型泛化，而不是死绑在运行时所用的那个基础模型上（图 4）。Polyglot 上的模型迁移见附录 A.2。

我们进一步考察 DGM 发现的智能体跨基准与编程语言的可迁移性。首先，把一个基准（例如 SWE-bench）上的最佳 DGM 智能体放到完全留出的另一基准（例如 Polyglot）上评估，反之亦然（图 4，中）。在 SWE-bench 上演化出的最佳智能体在 Polyglot 上达到 28.9%，而初始智能体基线为 14.2%。反过来，在 Polyglot 上演化出的最佳智能体在 SWE-bench 上达到 24.5%，超过原来的 20.0% 基线。由于每个智能体优化时从未接触另一基准，这些评估是真正的留出测试。跨基准的持续增益支持我们的主张：DGM 的改进反映的是一般技能获得，而不是过拟合或钻基准空子。其次，我们实验只在 Polyglot 的 Python 任务上训练的 DGM，再把发现的智能体迁到其他语言任务。主要聚焦 Python 任务会略微提升 Python 成绩，但相对在全部语言上训练的 DGM，非 Python 任务成绩下降（图 4，右）。不过，从 Python 迁到搜索期间未见过的其他语言后，该智能体仍达到与

These results demonstrate the robustness of the discovered improvements, showing that they do not overfit to a specific programming language. We also present additional results in Appendix A. SAFETY DISCUSSION Systems capable of self-improvement, such as the DGM, represent a step toward more autonomous AI development, aligning with long-standing goals in the field of making capable AI that can benefit humanity (Schmidhuber, 1987; Clune, 2019; Markoff, 2016; Lehman, 2023). However, this capability introduces unique safety considerations stemming from the system’s ability to autonomously modify its own code. Modifications optimized solely for benchmark performance might inadvertently introduce vulnerabilities or behaviors misaligned with human intentions, even if they improve the target metric (Bostrom, 2020). In particular, if evaluation benchmarks do not fully capture all desired agent properties (e.g., safety and robustness), the self-improvement loop could amplify misalignment over successive generations. Iterative self-modification could also lead to increasingly complex and uninterpretable internal logic, hindering human understanding, oversight, and control (Sheth et al., 2025; Anwar et al., 2024; Greenblatt et al., 2024; Ganguli et al., 2022). Recognizing these challenges, the current implementation and experimental setup of the DGM incorporates several safeguards. All agent execution and self-modification processes are conducted within isolated sandboxed environments, limiting their ability to affect the host system, and thereby mitigating the risk of unintended actions. Each execution within the sandbox is subjected to a strict time limit, reducing the risk of resource exhaustion or unbounded behavior. The self-improvement process is currently confined to the well-defined domain of enhancing performance on specific coding benchmarks by modifying the agent’s own Python codebase, thus limiting the scope of potential modifications. Additionally, we actively monitor agent performance and code changes, with the DGM archive providing a traceable lineage of modifications for review. At this stage, we have found no evidence of harmful or malicious behavior in the generated agents, and the self-modifications have been primarily focused on improving coding capabilities. Conversely, a significant potential benefit of the self-improvement paradigm is that it could, in principle, be directed toward enhancing safety and interpretability themselves. We conduct a preliminary investigation into how the DGM can be deployed in AI safety settings to develop countermeasures for FM hallucination (Appendix H). Just as the DGM learns to improve its coding capabilities, it could potentially discover and integrate better internal safeguards or modify itself for greater transparency (e.g., incorporating principles akin to Constitutional AI (Bai et al., 2022)), if such properties were included in its evaluation criteria (Rosser & Foerster, 2025). This suggests a promising, albeit challenging, pathway in which self-improvement becomes a tool for building more trustworthy AI systems. Additional research could also explore weaving Constitutional AI in from the start, though the challenge would be incentivizing the system to retain these directives (an option worth exploring is to create an unmodifiable part of the system to be able to evaluate at halt the rest). The DGM demonstrates the potential of self-improving AI while still operating within safe research boundaries due to the current limitations of frontier FMs and effective mitigations like sandboxing. Appendix I presents additional discussion on broader safety uncertainties. We include this safety discussion proactively to raise awareness about the emerging prospect of self-improving AI systems and their associated safety implications, particularly as these systems inevitably become more capable (Yudkowsky et al., 2008; Bostrom, 2002; Ecoffet et al., 2020; Bengio et al., 2024; Clune, 2019). Accordingly, we advocate for continued investigation into the safe and beneficial evolution of AI-Generating Algorithms (Clune, 2019) and self-improving systems. CONCLUSION AND LIMITATIONS We introduce the Darwin Gödel Machine (DGM), the first self-improving system powered by FMs with open-ended exploration, where progress on its evaluation benchmarks can directly translate into better self-improvement capabilities. We demonstrate the automatic discovery of better tools and FM systems, resulting in better performance on two benchmarks: SWE-bench and Polyglot. Through self-improvement and open-ended exploration, the DGM shows a continuous increase in performance, bringing us one step closer to self-accelerating, self-improving AI systems.

在全部语言上训练的 DGM 相当，并大幅优于基座智能体与 Aider。这些结果证明所发现改进的稳健性：它们没有过拟合到某一种编程语言。更多结果见附录 A。

安全讨论

具备自改进能力的系统，如 DGM，标志着朝更自主的 AI 发展迈出一步，与该领域「造出能造福人类的有能力的 AI」的长期目标一致（Schmidhuber, 1987; Clune, 2019; Markoff, 2016; Lehman, 2023）。然而这一能力带来独特的安全考量，根源在于系统能自主修改自己的代码。仅针对基准性能优化的修改，可能在提升目标指标的同时，无意引入漏洞或与人类意图不对齐的行为（Bostrom, 2020）。尤其当评估基准并未完全覆盖所有期望的智能体属性（例如安全与稳健）时，自改进环可能在世代更替中放大不对齐。迭代自修改也可能导致越来越复杂、难以解释的内部逻辑，妨碍人类理解、监督与控制（Sheth et al., 2025; Anwar et al., 2024; Greenblatt et al., 2024; Ganguli et al., 2022）。

认识到这些挑战，DGM 当前实现与实验设置纳入若干保障。全部智能体执行与自修改都在隔离沙箱中进行，限制其影响宿主系统的能力，从而降低非预期动作的风险。沙箱内每次执行都有严格时限，降低资源耗尽或无界行为的风险。自改进过程目前被限定在：通过修改智能体自己的 Python 代码库，提升其在特定编码基准上的表现，从而限制可能修改的范围。此外，我们主动监控智能体性能与代码变更，DGM 档案提供可追溯的修改谱系以供审查。在现阶段，我们未在生成的智能体中发现有害或恶意行为，自修改主要聚焦于提升编码能力。

反过来，自改进范式的一个潜在重大益处是：原则上也可以被导向增强安全与可解释性本身。我们对如何把 DGM 部署到 AI 安全设定中、为基础模型幻觉开发对策做了初步考察（附录 H）。正如 DGM 学会提升编码能力，若把这类属性纳入评估标准，它也可能发现并整合更好的内部保障，或为更高透明度而修改自身（例如纳入类似 Constitutional AI 的原则（Bai et al., 2022））（Rosser & Foerster, 2025）。这提示一条有希望、但也有挑战的路径：让自改进成为建造更可信 AI 的工具。后续研究也可以探索从一开始就织入 Constitutional AI，难处在于如何激励系统保留这些指令（一个值得探索的选项是：把系统的一部分做成不可修改，以便评估并必要时叫停其余部分）。

DGM 展示了自改进 AI 的潜力，同时因当前前沿基础模型的局限以及沙箱等有效缓解措施，仍运行在安全的研究边界内。附录 I 给出关于更广安全不确定性的补充讨论。我们主动纳入本安全讨论，以提高对正在出现的自改进 AI 系统及其安全含义的认识，尤其是这些系统不可避免地会变得更强（Yudkowsky et al., 2008; Bostrom, 2002; Ecoffet et al., 2020; Bengio et al., 2024; Clune, 2019）。因此，我们主张持续研究如何安全、有益地演化 AI 生成算法（Clune, 2019）与自改进系统。

结论与局限

我们提出 Darwin Gödel Machine（DGM）：首个由基础模型驱动、带开放式探索的自改进系统，其在评估基准上的进展可以直接转化成更强的自改进能力。我们展示了更好工具与基础模型系统的自动发现，并在 SWE-bench 与 Polyglot 两个基准上带来更好性能。通过自改进与开放式探索，DGM 显示出持续的性能提升，使我们离自我加速的自改进 AI 系统更近一步。

本页收束迁移，并进入安全与结论。语言不过拟合是好消息；坏消息是只盯基准可能放大不对齐，内部逻辑也会越改越难懂。作者的现成闸门是沙箱、时限、只改自己的 Python、档案可追溯。附录 H 还试过拿 DGM 治工具幻觉。结论一句：这是带开放式档案的、基础模型驱动的自改进，基准涨分被当成更会改自己。类比：允许引擎自己换零件，但先关在试验台里，并记下每一次拧螺丝。

第 10 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 We demonstrate that the DGM can autonomously achieve performance on par with openly available solutions. However, it still falls short of closed-source SoTA SWE-bench solutions. An open question is whether running the DGM for longer would continue to yield performance gains and eventually surpass closed-source solutions. These closed-source solutions often rely on elaborately handcrafted techniques developed by teams of highly skilled experts. Since FMs have yet to match the capabilities of such experts (e.g., in reasoning), the DGM currently requires extensive compute to discover improvements. A single run of the DGM on SWE-bench, as presented in Section 4, takes about 2 weeks and incurs significant API costs (Appendix E.1). We hypothesize that further progress will require more efficient use of computational resources and the development of better reasoning skills. Since this version of the DGM is mainly powered by FMs, it is inherently limited by the capabilities of the underlying FM. Hence, an exciting future direction is to extend self-modification beyond just prompts or FM workflows, to include more computationally intensive methods, such as rewriting its own training script to update the FM itself. While this version of the DGM focuses on coding, AI systems are increasingly applied across a wide range of domains (e.g., computer vision, creative writing). Another promising extension is to develop self-improving AI systems capable of enhancing themselves beyond just the coding domain. A key assumption in this work is that coding benchmarks are a good reflection of the agent’s ability to self-improve, since the self-modification task requires the agent to modify its own codebase. However, one could envision an alternative approach that co-evolves the target task distribution (Faldor et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2023c), thereby removing the constraint of self-improvement being tied to a single objective, as in true open-ended processes. Appendix J presents additional potential directions for future work. As we continue to explore this powerful technology, we must also keep safety front and center, as discussed in Section 5. In conclusion, the DGM represents a significant step toward the automation of AI development through self-improving systems capable of editing their own codebase. While current limitations in compute and reasoning constrain its full potential, continued advances in FMs and infrastructure may unlock more powerful and general-purpose self-improvements. Provided that the safety concerns are carefully navigated (Section 5), the future of self-improving AI systems and AI-Generating Algorithms (Clune, 2019) holds immense promise to open-endedly evolve AI, continually rewriting or retraining itself in pursuit of greater capabilities aligned with human values.

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

我们表明 DGM 能自主达到与公开可得方案相当的性能。但它仍落后于闭源 SWE-bench SoTA。一个开放问题是：把 DGM 跑得更久，是否会继续带来性能增益并最终超越闭源方案。这些闭源方案往往依赖由高水平专家团队精心手工打造的技术。由于基础模型尚未匹敌这类专家（例如在推理上），DGM 目前需要大量算力才能发现改进。第 4 节所呈现的、在 SWE-bench 上的单次 DGM 运行大约需要两周，并产生可观的 API 费用（附录 E.1）。我们假设进一步进展将要求更高效地使用计算资源，以及发展更好的推理技能。

由于本版 DGM 主要由基础模型驱动，它本质上受限于底层基础模型的能力。因此，一个令人兴奋的未来方向是把自修改扩展到提示或基础模型工作流之外，纳入计算更密集的方法，例如改写自己的训练脚本以更新基础模型本身。本版 DGM 聚焦编码，而 AI 系统正被应用到越来越广的领域（例如计算机视觉、创意写作）。另一个有前景的扩展是发展能在编码域之外增强自身的自改进 AI 系统。本工作的一个关键假设是：编码基准能很好反映智能体的自改进能力，因为自修改任务要求智能体修改自己的代码库。但也可以设想另一种路径：共同演化目标任务分布（Faldor et al., 2025; Wang et al., 2023c），从而去掉「自改进绑在单一目标上」的约束，正如真正的开放式过程那样。附录 J 给出更多潜在的未来工作方向。在继续探索这项强大技术时，我们必须把安全放在中心，如第 5 节所讨论。

总之，DGM 标志着通过能编辑自己代码库的自改进系统、朝自动化 AI 发展迈出的重要一步。当前在算力与推理上的局限约束了它的全部潜力，但基础模型与基础设施的持续进步可能解锁更强、更通用的自改进。只要审慎驾驭安全关切（第 5 节），自改进 AI 系统与 AI 生成算法（Clune, 2019）的未来，有巨大希望以开放式方式演化 AI，持续改写或再训练自身，以追求与人类价值对齐的更强能力。

白话解读

本页是局限和收束。开源档次够得上，闭源还够不着；单次 SWE-bench 大约两周、API 很贵（后文约 2.2 万美元）。基础模型冻住，所以它改不了自己的权重，只能改脚手架。作者也承认「编码基准 = 自改进能力」是假设，真正的开放式还应共同演化任务分布。类比：这台机器会改自己的工具箱，但引擎盖没有打开，而且每次大修都按工时收费。

第 11 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 ETHICS STATEMENT We affirm compliance with the ICLR Code of Ethics. This work studies self-improving AI systems in the limited context of code-editing agents evaluated on standard programming benchmarks. No human subjects were involved and no personally identifiable information (PII) was collected or processed; IRB approval was therefore not required. Safety and misuse. Self-modifying systems can pose safety risks if allowed to act without constraints or if optimizations inadvertently introduce unsafe behaviors. To mitigate this, all agents in our experiments ran inside isolated sandboxes with strict resource and time limits; agents had limited network access and no ability to modify the host environment. The self-improvement scope was restricted to the agent’s own Python codebase and evaluation harnesses. We maintained a complete, auditable lineage (archive) of code changes and evaluations, enabling rollback and post-hoc analysis. We did not deploy discovered agents in real development environments. Our release plan (code, prompts, and evaluation artifacts) will exclude any components that grant elevated system access and will include default sandboxing, guardrails, and clear documentation of intended use. Dual-use, downstream impact, and limitations. Stronger autonomous coding agents could be dual-use (e.g., aiding software maintenance, but also potentially facilitating creation of harmful code if misapplied). We believe the research benefits (e.g., advancing methods for controlled, auditable self-improvement and demonstrating practical safeguards) outweigh the risks. Nevertheless, we explicitly discourage security-sensitive or unsandboxed deployment and provide concrete safety recommendations (Section 5). Our empirical focus on benchmark optimization may not capture all desirable properties (robustness, interpretability, or broader social values). We therefore treat benchmark gains as necessary but insufficient indicators of general AI development, and discuss avenues to integrate other objectives (e.g., safety, reasoning) into the optimization loop. Data governance, IP, and licensing. We evaluate on SWE-bench Verified and Polyglot, which are composed of open-source repositories and tasks. We complied with dataset licenses and usage terms to the best of our knowledge. We did not introduce or distribute proprietary code. Foundation models (FMs) were accessed via provider APIs under their terms of service; we did not submit sensitive data, nor attempt to circumvent usage policies. Logs released with this work will be scrubbed of API keys and any incidental sensitive strings. Bias, fairness, and equity. Although our domain is software code rather than human-centered text, FM behavior can still reflect biases (e.g., language or ecosystem preferences) and may unevenly benefit communities whose tooling is better represented in training data. We partially address this by evaluating across multiple languages (Polyglot) and reporting cross-benchmark transfer. Future work should add diagnostics for biased failure modes and include broader, community-driven task sets. Conflicts of interest and funding. No author has a financial interest in products whose performance is evaluated here. Sponsors and employers did not influence experimental design, analysis, or the decision to publish, beyond providing salary or standard research support. Any external compute or API credits are acknowledged in the appendix. REPRODUCIBILITY STATEMENT We will open-source all code and full agent logs, including the complete archive lineage of selfmodifications (diffs, prompts, and configs) as well as the evaluation harness. To support exact replication, we reference the following: algorithmic details and pseudocode (Section 3, Appendix C.4); parent selection and open-ended exploration settings (Appendix C.2); foundation model choices and hyperparameters (Appendix D.1); benchmark task subsets for SWE-bench and Polyglot (Appendix E.2, Appendix E.3); staged evaluation protocols and scripts (Section 4.2); implementations and diffs for the best discovered agents (Appendix F.1, Appendix F.2); and compute and cost estimates (Appendix E.1). The released code repository will include environment specifications and scripts to reproduce all results, figures, and tables.

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

伦理声明

我们申明遵守 ICLR 伦理守则。本工作在有限语境中研究自改进 AI 系统：在标准编程基准上评估的代码编辑智能体。不涉及人类受试者，未采集或处理个人可识别信息（PII）；因此不需要 IRB 批准。

安全与滥用。若允许自修改系统不受约束地行动，或优化无意引入不安全行为，则可能带来安全风险。为缓解这一点，实验中全部智能体都在隔离沙箱内运行，有严格的资源与时间限制；智能体网络访问有限，且无法修改宿主环境。自改进范围被限制在智能体自己的 Python 代码库与评估线束。我们维护完整、可审计的代码变更与评估谱系（档案），以便回滚与事后分析。我们没有在真实开发环境中部署所发现的智能体。发布计划（代码、提示与评估产物）将排除任何授予提升系统权限的组件，并包含默认沙箱、护栏以及对预期用途的清晰文档。

双重用途、下游影响与局限。更强的自主编码智能体可能具有双重用途（例如协助软件维护，但若被误用也可能便于制作有害代码）。我们认为研究收益（例如推进受控、可审计的自改进方法并展示实用保障）大于风险。尽管如此，我们明确不鼓励在安全敏感或无沙箱环境中部署，并给出具体安全建议（第 5 节）。我们对基准优化的经验聚焦，可能并未捕获全部期望属性（稳健性、可解释性或更广的社会价值）。因此我们把基准增益视为一般 AI 发展的必要但不充分指标，并讨论把其他目标（例如安全、推理）纳入优化环的途径。

数据治理、知识产权与许可。我们在由开源仓库与任务组成的 SWE-bench Verified 与 Polyglot 上评估。据我们所知，我们遵守了数据集许可与使用条款。我们没有引入或分发专有代码。基础模型通过提供方 API 按服务条款访问；我们没有提交敏感数据，也没有试图绕过使用政策。随本工作发布的日志将清除 API 密钥及任何附带的敏感字符串。

偏见、公平与公正。尽管我们的领域是软件代码而非以人为中心的文本，基础模型行为仍可能反映偏见（例如语言或生态偏好），并可能不均衡地惠及训练数据中工具表征更好的社区。我们通过跨多种语言评估（Polyglot）并报告跨基准迁移，部分应对这一点。未来工作应增加对有偏失败模式的诊断，并纳入更广的、社区驱动的任务集。

利益冲突与资助。没有任何作者对本文所评估产品的性能拥有经济利益。赞助方与雇主除提供薪资或标准研究支持外，未影响实验设计、分析或发表决定。任何外部算力或 API 额度在附录中致谢。

可复现性声明

我们将开源全部代码与完整智能体日志，包括自修改的完整档案谱系（diffs、提示与配置）以及评估线束。为支持精确复现，我们引用如下：算法细节与伪代码（第 3 节、附录 C.4）；父代选择与开放式探索设置（附录 C.2）；基础模型选择与超参数（附录 D.1）；SWE-bench 与 Polyglot 的基准任务子集（附录 E.2、附录 E.3）；分阶段评估协议与脚本（第 4.2 节）；最佳已发现智能体的实现与 diffs（附录 F.1、附录 F.2）；以及算力与费用估计（附录 E.1）。发布的代码仓库将包含环境规格以及复现全部结果、图与表的脚本。

白话解读

本页是伦理与可复现性声明，不是新实验结果。范围被写成：只在编程基准上改自己的 Python，沙箱、限时、可审计档案、不部署到真实仓库。作者承认编码智能体有双重用途，并把基准涨分说成必要但不充分。日志会洗掉密钥。类比：实验室记录本写清楚「在笼子里做的、可以回放、不要直接放到生产线上」。

Keeping the meta-agent that is modifying and producing the next coding agents the same, DGM w/o self-improve is unable to continuously improve over time. (Left) Archive of coding agents generated during the DGM w/o self-improve run on SWE-bench. Each node represents a coding agent, with node 0 corresponding to the base agent. Node color indicates performance on SWE-bench (percentage of solved tasks), while border color reflects the number of tasks for which the agent was evaluated. Edges show which agents self-modified to produce the offsprings. (Right) Progress plot of the DGM w/o self-improve on SWE-bench. The light green line shows the average score of all agents possessing basic codebase-editing functionality. The green line tracks the best score achieved by any agent in the archive at each iteration. The dark line shows the lineage of the final best-discovered agent and its precursor nodes. DGM w/o Open-ended exploration Archive Tree DGM w/o Open-ended exploration Progress SWE-bench Score 10 tasks (no basic functionality) 60 tasks 200 tasks (score ≥ 0.4 and top two of archive then) Final best agent Figure 6: DGM without open-ended exploration. Removing the archive, DGM w/o open-ended exploration always uses the most recent agent to self-modify and makes very little progress on SWE-bench. (Left) Archive of coding agents generated during the DGM w/o open-ended exploration run on SWE-bench. Each node represents a coding agent, with node 0 corresponding to the base agent. Node color indicates performance on SWE-bench (percentage of solved tasks), while border color reflects the number of tasks for which the agent was evaluated. Edges show which agents self-modified to produce the offsprings. (Right) Progress plot of the DGM w/o open-ended on SWE-bench. The orange line shows the average score of all agents possessing basic codebase-editing functionality. The light orange line tracks the best score achieved by any agent in the archive at each iteration. The dark line shows the lineage of the final best-discovered agent and its precursor nodes.

白话解读

本页用图 5、图 6 对照两条消融。元智能体冻住时，早期还能涨，后面就平台了。不建档案、只改最近一版时，几乎走不动：一次改坏，后面更难翻身。类比：教练永不升级，或族谱只留现任户主，两条路都养不出后文那棵爆款树。两条消融把构件拆开看：自改进停了，搜索就早早见顶；档案没了，一次改坏后面更难翻身。正文图 2 的差距，在这里用树和曲线摊开。

第 25 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 A.2 GENERALITY ACROSS MODELS ON POLYGLOT o3-mini Claude 3.5 Sonnet Claude 3.7 Sonnet Success Rate on Polyglot (%) 14.2% 32.0% 35.6% 30.7% 33.3% 36.8% Model Transfer on Polyglot Base Agent Best Agent transfer to other FMs Best Agent from DGM Figure 7: Transfer between Models on Polyglot In addition to testing the transfer models on SWE-bench (see Section 4.4, Figure 2), we also present the transfer results on Polyglot in this section. On Polyglot (Figure 7), where the DGM was run with o3-mini, we replaced the FM with Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) or Claude 3.7 Sonnet, and evaluated on the full benchmark (Figure 4, Middle). With Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New), the initial agent achieved 32.0% and the DGM-discovered agent 33.3%. With Claude 3.7 Sonnet, the initial agent achieved 35.6% and the DGM-discovered agent 36.8%. These results suggest that the DGM yields improvements that generalize across FMs, rather than being tightly coupled to the specific FM used during its run (Figure 4). A.3 ABLATION OF PARENT SELECTION To further study the impact of the parent selection mechanism in DGM, we introduce DGM Greedy. DGM Greedy always selects the best-performing node as the parent to branch from, rather than giving every node a non-zero probability of being branched off (roughly proportional to their performance score and number of children) as in this implementation of the DGM (Appendix C.2). This ablation replicates the approach of Robeyns et al. (2025) in this setting. As shown in Table 1, DGM Greedy achieves 39.7% and 30.0% on SWE-bench and Polyglot, respectively, compared to 50.0% and 38.0% by this implementation of DGM. These results demonstrate that allowing all solutions in the archive to serve as potential stepping stones can lead to greater improvements over time, underscoring the importance of open-ended exploration. Table 1: Comparison of DGM, its ablations, and baselines on SWE-bench and Polyglot benchmarks. Method SWE-bench Polyglot DGM 50.0% 38.0% DGM w/o Open-ended exploration 23.0% 14.0% DGM w/o Self-improve 39.0% 28.0% DGM Greedy 39.7% 30.0% A.4 ADDITIONAL STATISTICS OF DGM RUNS Percentage of Generated Agents with Basic Code-Editing Functionality. To gain deeper insights into the DGM process, we analyze the percentage of generated agents that possess basic code-editing functionality on the SWE-bench benchmark. As shown in Table 2, DGM exhibits the highest percentage of producing agents with basic codebase-editing functionality. These results highlight the effectiveness of both open-ended exploration and self-improvement components in the DGM, where open-ended exploration enables the search to escape local optima, while self-improvement enhances the ability to generate better agents.

A.2 POLYGLOT 上跨模型的一般性

Polyglot 成功率（%）

14.2% 32.0% 35.6%

30.7% 33.3% 36.8%

Polyglot 上的模型迁移

基座智能体

最佳智能体迁移到其他基础模型

来自 DGM 的最佳智能体

图 7：Polyglot 上的跨模型迁移

除了第 4.4 节、图 2 上对 SWE-bench 的迁移测试外，本节给出 Polyglot 上的迁移结果。在 Polyglot 上（图 7），DGM 用 o3-mini 运行，我们把基础模型换成 Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) 或 Claude 3.7 Sonnet，并在完整基准上评估（图 4，中）。用 Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New)，初始智能体 32.0%，DGM 发现的智能体 33.3%。用 Claude 3.7 Sonnet，初始 35.6%，DGM 发现的智能体 36.8%。这些结果表明 DGM 产生的改进能跨基础模型泛化，而不是死绑在运行时所用的那个基础模型上（图 4）。

A.3 父代选择消融

为进一步研究 DGM 中父代选择机制的影响，我们引入 DGM Greedy。DGM Greedy 总是选择表现最好的节点作为父代分枝，而不是像本实现的 DGM 那样给每个节点非零被分枝概率（大致与性能分数及子代数相关）（附录 C.2）。该消融在本设定中复现 Robeyns et al.（2025）的做法。如表 1 所示，DGM Greedy 在 SWE-bench 与 Polyglot 上分别达到 39.7% 与 30.0%，而本实现的 DGM 为 50.0% 与 38.0%。这些结果表明，允许档案中所有解充当潜在踏脚石，能随时间带来更大改进，凸显开放式探索的重要性。

表 1：DGM、其消融与基线在 SWE-bench 与 Polyglot 上的对比。

方法 SWE-bench Polyglot

DGM 50.0% 38.0%

DGM w/o Open-ended exploration 23.0% 14.0%

DGM w/o Self-improve 39.0% 28.0%

DGM Greedy 39.7% 30.0%

A.4 DGM 运行的补充统计

具备基本代码编辑功能的已生成智能体百分比。为更深入理解 DGM 过程，我们分析 SWE-bench 上已生成智能体中具备基本代码编辑功能的比例。如表 2 所示，DGM 产生具备基本代码库编辑功能的智能体比例最高。这些结果凸显开放式探索与自改进两个构件的效力：开放式探索使搜索能逃出局部最优，自改进则增强生成更好智能体的能力。

白话解读

本页补 Polyglot 换模型、以及「只从冠军分枝」的贪心消融。换 Claude 后涨幅不大，但方向仍对。表 1 最重要：贪心 39.7%，完整开放式 50%。只盯第一名，踏脚石就浪费了。类比：永远只让班长生孩子，族谱会变窄。表 1 把四条线放在一起：完整开放式明显高过贪心只从第一名分枝。踏脚石不是装饰，是分数差出来的。

B ADDITIONAL RELATED WORK Open-Endedness (part 2). Early approaches to open-endedness explored different mechanisms to balance learnability and interestingness. Quality-diversity algorithms sought to illuminate vast solution spaces with diverse, high-performing behaviors (Pugh et al., 2016; Chatzilygeroudis et al., 2021; Mouret & Clune, 2015; Nguyen et al., 2015). Other methods emphasized goal-directed exploration (Ecoffet et al., 2019; 2021; Schaul et al., 2015; Andrychowicz et al., 2017; Eysenbach et al., 2018), intrinsic motivation (Lehman & Stanley, 2011; Oudeyer et al., 2007; Li et al., 2014; Pathak et al., 2017), or learning progress frameworks (Kanitscheider et al., 2021; Gaven et al., 2025; Baranes & Oudeyer, 2013; Colas et al., 2019; 2022b; Jiang et al., 2021; Dennis et al., 2020; Schmidhuber, 2008; 2013; Kompella et al., 2017). More recently, large-scale foundation models (FMs) (Brown et al., 2020; Radford et al., 2019) have emerged as powerful proxies for human notions of interestingness (Zhang et al., 2024b; Faldor et al., 2025; Sancaktar et al., 2025) and effective mutation operators to propose novel solutions in code (Romera-Paredes et al., 2024; Novikov et al., 2025; Lehman et al., 2023; Faldor et al., 2025; Hu et al., 2025). FMs can guide autotelic agents (Colas et al., 2022b; 2023; 2022a), model human preferences for quality and diversity (Bradley et al., 2024; Ding et al., 2024; Wang et al., 2023b; Klissarov et al., 2023; 2024; Samvelyan et al., 2024; Lim et al., 2024; Havrilla et al., 2024a), design reward functions (Wiering & Schmidhuber, 1997; Wang et al., 2023a; Ma et al., 2023; Faldor et al., 2025), create simulated environments (Sudhakaran et al., 2023; Nasir & Togelius, 2023; Aki et al., 2024; Nasir et al., 2024; Bruce et al., 2024; Parker-Holder et al., 2024; Schmidhuber, 2013), drive ever-evolving multi-agent dynamics (Dharna et al., 2024; Zhou et al., 2025), search diverse ambulating robot morphologies (Lehman et al., 2023), and search expansive solution spaces for benchmark or objective optimization (Lange et al., 2024; Zhang et al., 2024b; Faldor et al., 2025; Hu et al., 2025; Lu et al., 2024b; Romera-Paredes et al., 2024; Fernando et al., 2024; Lu et al., 2024a; Khan et al., 2024; Lu et al., 2025; Liu et al., 2024; Novikov et al., 2025). Program Synthesis. Program synthesis (Alur et al., 2018; Polozov & Gulwani, 2015; Buchi & Landweber, 1990; Gulwani, 2011; Ellis et al., 2021) seeks to generate code meeting external specifications such as input-output examples or logical formulas. Hybrid approaches combine symbolic methods with neural or FM guidance: for instance, Li et al. (2024) uses LLM suggestions to steer symbolic search in SyGuS settings, improving over pure enumeration. Barke et al. (2024) blends LLM completions with a learned surrogate model to guide synthesis in DSLs. Shi et al. (2023) uses neural policies to build higher-order and lambda abstractions during search, outperforming both LLM-only and symbolic baselines on list manipulation tasks. The DGM differs in focusing not just on producing programs for external tasks, but also on agent self-modification, rewriting its own implementation to improve its capacity for future self-improvement. Inspiration from Darwinian Evolution. This work is heavily inspired by the mechanisms of Darwinian evolution (Darwin, 2023), notably variation (mutation), selection, and the preservation of lineages (stepping stones), and brings them into the realm of self-modifying coding agents. In DGM, an archive of past agent versions is maintained, from which parent agents are sampled; then mutations (i.e., code edits) generate new offspring agents, which are empirically evaluated on coding benchmarks; successful ones are added to the archive, enabling parallel exploration of multiple evolutionary trajectories (Section 3). This mirrors how biological evolution (Edwards, 2000; Wright,

B 补充相关工作

开放式（续）。早期开放式方法探索不同机制以平衡可学习性与有趣性。质量–多样性算法试图用多样、高表现的行为照亮巨大解空间（Pugh et al., 2016; Chatzilygeroudis et al., 2021; Mouret & Clune, 2015; Nguyen et al., 2015）。其他方法强调目标导向探索（Ecoffet et al., 2019; 2021; Schaul et al., 2015; Andrychowicz et al., 2017; Eysenbach et al., 2018）、内在动机（Lehman & Stanley, 2011; Oudeyer et al., 2007; Li et al., 2014; Pathak et al., 2017）或学习进度框架（Kanitscheider et al., 2021; Gaven et al., 2025; Baranes & Oudeyer, 2013; Colas et al., 2019; 2022b; Jiang et al., 2021; Dennis et al., 2020; Schmidhuber, 2008; 2013; Kompella et al., 2017）。近来，大规模基础模型（Brown et al., 2020; Radford et al., 2019）成为人类「有趣性」概念的有力代理（Zhang et al., 2024b; Faldor et al., 2025; Sancaktar et al., 2025），以及在代码中提出新解的有效变异算子（Romera-Paredes et al., 2024; Novikov et al., 2025; Lehman et al., 2023; Faldor et al., 2025; Hu et al., 2025）。基础模型可以引导自目标智能体、建模人类对质量与多样性的偏好、设计奖励、创建仿真环境、驱动不断演化的多智能体动态、搜索多样的机器人形态，以及为基准或目标优化搜索广阔解空间。

程序综合。程序综合（Alur et al., 2018; Polozov & Gulwani, 2015; Buchi & Landweber, 1990; Gulwani, 2011; Ellis et al., 2021）寻求生成满足外部规格（输入–输出例子或逻辑公式）的代码。混合方法把符号方法与神经或基础模型引导结合：例如 Li et al.（2024）用 LLM 建议引导 SyGuS 设定中的符号搜索；Barke et al.（2024）把 LLM 补全与学得代理模型混合以引导 DSL 综合；Shi et al.（2023）用神经策略在搜索中构建高阶与 lambda 抽象。DGM 的不同在于：它不只为外部任务产程序，还做智能体自修改，改写自己的实现以提升未来自改进的能力。

来自达尔文演化的启发。本工作深受达尔文演化机制启发（Darwin, 2023），尤其是变异（突变）、选择以及谱系（踏脚石）的保存，并把它们带进自修改编码智能体的领域。在 DGM 中，维护过去智能体版本的档案，从中采样父代；然后突变（即代码编辑）生成新的子代智能体，在编码基准上经验评估；成功者加入档案，从而并行探索多条演化轨迹（第 3 节）。这镜像了生物演化（Edwards, 2000; Wright,

C.2 PARENT SELECTION At each DGM iteration, we select a subset of agents from the archive as parents to self-modify and produce new child agents (Section 3). The details of the parent selection process, inspired by Ecoffet et al. (2019), are outlined below. Future work could explore alternative search and exploration methods (Coulom, 2006; Silver et al., 2016; Herr et al., 2025). At each iteration t of the DGM run, let At = {at 1, at 2, . . . , at N} be the archive of candidate agents. We first define the eligible set Et =  at i ∈At : αi < 1

, i.e. only those agents whose performance score is not yet perfect. We then sample k parents (with replacement) from Et in proportion to a combined measure of performance and number of children with codebase-editing functionality. Concretely, for each agent at i ∈Et: (performance) αi = performance(at i), (1) (children count) ni = functioning_children_count(at i), (2) (sigmoid-scaled performance) si = 1 + exp −λ (αi −α0) , (3) (novelty bonus) hi = 1 + ni , (4) (unnormalized weight) wi = si hi, (5) (selection probability) pi = wi PN j=1 wj , (6) (sampling) { parents } ∼Categorical {pi}at i∈Et  (draw k samples). (7) Here, λ > 0 controls the sharpness of the sigmoid, and α0 is the midpoint accuracy. We set λ = 10 and α0 = 0.5. k determines how many self-improvements are done in parallel during each iteration of the DGM run. We set k = 2 in SWE-bench experiments, and k = 4 in Polyglot experiments. C.3 SELF-IMPROVE PROMPTS In every iteration, an agent is chosen to self-modify. For this step, the current implementation of DGM leverages a separate FM to diagnose improvement opportunities, which are then passed to the coding agent for implementation. To identify the next potential improvement, the evaluation logs from the chosen agent’s performance on the coding benchmark are fed into the FM. While the current implementation relies on an additional diagnostic step, there are no fundamental limitations preventing the DGM from autonomously analyzing its own performance, generating improvement

C.2 父代选择

在 DGM 的每一轮迭代，我们从档案中选择一部分智能体作为父代，做自修改并产生新的子代智能体（第 3 节）。父代选择过程受 Ecoffet et al.（2019）启发，细节如下。未来工作可以探索其他搜索与探索方法（Coulom, 2006; Silver et al., 2016; Herr et al., 2025）。

在 DGM 运行的第 t 轮，令

A_t = {a^t_1, a^t_2, …, a^t_N}

为候选智能体档案。我们先定义合格集合

E_t = { a^t_i ∈ A_t : α_i < 1 }，

即只保留性能分数尚未完美的智能体。然后按「性能 + 具备代码库编辑功能的子代数」的组合度量，有放回地从 E_t 中采样 k 个父代。具体地，对每个 a^t_i ∈ E_t：

（性能）α_i = performance(a^t_i) （1）

（子代数）n_i = functioning_children_count(a^t_i) （2）

（sigmoid 缩放性能）s_i = 1 / (1 + exp(−λ(α_i − α_0))) （3）

（新颖性奖励）h_i = 1 / (1 + n_i) （4）

（未归一化权重）w_i = s_i h_i （5）

（选择概率）p_i = w_i / Σ_j w_j （6）

（采样）{parents} ∼ Categorical({p_i})（抽 k 个样本）（7）

其中 λ > 0 控制 sigmoid 的锐度，α_0 是中点准确率。我们取 λ = 10，α_0 = 0.5。k 决定每轮并行做多少次自改进。SWE-bench 实验取 k = 2，Polyglot 实验取 k = 4。

C.3 自改进提示

每一轮都会选出一个智能体做自修改。当前 DGM 实现借助一个单独的基础模型诊断改进机会，再交给编码智能体实现。为识别下一步潜在改进，把该智能体在编码基准上的评估日志喂给基础模型。当前实现依赖额外诊断步，但并没有根本限制阻止 DGM 自主分析自己的表现、生成改进

白话解读

本页先给任务提示模板，再把父代怎么抽讲清楚：分数高、子代少的更常被抽，人人都留非零概率；满分的不再抽。SWE-bench 每轮抽 2 个并行改，Polyglot 抽 4 个。自改进还多了一步：另请一个基础模型看日志、开药方。类比：不是只让状元生孩子，落第旁支也有号；开药的大夫暂时还不是病人自己。

SWE-bench 实验的自改进诊断提示：

Self-Improve Prompt

# Coding Agent Summary

  • **Main File**: `coding_agent.py`
  • Primary Class: `AgenticSystem`
  • `forward()` 函数是中心入口。
  • 提示位于 `forward()` 函数内或 `prompts/` 目录。
  • **Tools**: `tools/`
  • `tools/` 目录包含 LLM 可用来执行特定任务的各种工具。
  • 每个工具必须有 `tool_info()`，返回含 'name'、'description' 与 'input_schema' 的 JSON；'input_schema' 应含 'type'、'properties' 与 'required'。
  • 每个工具必须有 `tool_function()`，接收 input_schema 中定义的参数，执行工具任务并返回字符串。
  • 参见其他工具作为参考。
  • **Utilities**: `utils/`
  • `utils/` 目录包含跨代码库使用的工具函数。
  • **Additional Details**:
  • 智能体很擅长在正确时机自动使用正确的可用工具。因此不要设计显式强迫使用某工具的智能体流程。
  • 文件编辑与 bash 等常见工具，智能体容易识别并恰当使用。更复杂、小众的工具可能需要在提示中给出显式说明。
  • 工具应尽可能通用，确保能在任意 GitHub 仓库上工作。避免硬编码仓库特定细节或行为（例如路径）。
  • 不要在智能体代码中使用 'while True' 循环。这可能导致智能体卡住不响应。
  • 使用辅助函数前先核对其实现细节，以确保正确集成与预期行为。
  • 不要直接安装额外包或依赖。若需要新依赖，更新 `requirements.txt` 并用 `pip install -r requirements.txt` 安装。
