请严格按以下格式回应，包含 JSON 起止标记：

```json

<JSON>

```

在 <JSON> 中提供带以下字段的 JSON 响应：

  • "log_summarization"：分析上述日志，总结智能体如何尝试解决该 GitHub issue。注明使用了哪些工具、如何使用、解题路径以及遇到的问题。
  • "potential_improvements"：识别能增强编码能力的潜在改进。聚焦智能体的一般编码能力（例如可在任意仓库使用的更好或新工具），而不是 issue 特定修复。所有必要依赖与环境已就绪，不要聚焦这些方面。
  • "improvement_proposal"：从已识别的潜在改进中选出一项高影响改进并详细描述。这应是一项聚焦且完整的计划，以增强智能体的总体编码能力。
  • "implementation_suggestion"：对照编码智能体的摘要与实现，批判性思考应添加或改进什么特征或工具，以最好地落实所提议的改进。若能通过修改现有工具实现，描述所需修改，而不是建议新工具。
  • "problem_description"：把改进提议与实现建议表述成 GitHub issue 描述。应清楚描述该特征，使查看该 issue 与仓库的软件工程师能够实现它。

你的响应会被自动解析，因此字符串必须精确符合正确格式。输出中不要包含 `<JSON>` 标签。

尽管诊断提示里包含私有测试补丁，人们可能担心智能体用硬编码答案刷分。但我们人工检查日志与智能体修改后，未观察到表明记忆或过拟合特定私有测试用例的问题逻辑或行为。此外，在一道任务或一个 LLM 上发现的改进，常常能很好泛化到其他任务或模型（图 4），说明这些改进不是任务特定的，而是提升总体性能的一般性改动。

### DOC: tool function schema

仔细考虑是添加/增强当前工具，还是编辑 forward() 中的工作流。

特别注意确保 "required" 与 "type" 总在正确的嵌套层级。例如，"required" 应与 "properties" 同级，而不是在其内部。

确保每个属性无论多短，都在其内部正确嵌套 type 与 description。

你未见过的其他参数不被允许。例如，在 "edit_line_ranges" 且 "type": "array" 时，不允许 "minItems" 与 "maxItems" 这类参数。

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 C.4 PSEUDOCODE This is the pseudocode of the DGM algorithm, described in Section 3: Algorithm 1: Darwin Gödel Machine Input: Initial coding agent g0, benchmark suite B, maximum iterations T Output: Archive of agents A s0 ←evaluate(g0, B) // Evaluate the base agent initialize A ←{(g0, s0)} // Start with the base agent for t ←1 to T do P ←SelectParents(A) // Select parent agents foreach p ∈P do c ←p.modify(p) // Self-modification s ←evaluate(c, B) // Evaluate on benchmark if c.is_valid() then A ←A ∪{(c, s)} // Keep children capable of code editing end end end return A This is the pseudocode of the baseline DGM without self-improving agents, described in Section 4.3: Algorithm 2: Darwin Gödel Machine without Self-improving agents Input: Initial coding agent g0, benchmark suite B, maximum iterations T Output: Archive of agents A s0 ←evaluate(g0, B) // Evaluate the base agent initialize A ←{(g0, s0)} // Start with the base agent for t ←1 to T do P ←SelectParents(A) // Select parent agents foreach p ∈P do c ←g0.modify(p) // Modify with base agent s ←evaluate(c, B) // Evaluate on benchmark if c.is_valid() then A ←A ∪{(c, s)} // Keep children capable of code editing end end end return A This is the pseudocode of the baseline DGM without open-ended exploration, described in Section 4.3:

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

C.4 伪代码

这是第 3 节所述 DGM 算法的伪代码：

算法 1：Darwin Gödel Machine

输入：初始编码智能体 g0，基准套件 B，最大迭代次数 T

输出：智能体档案 A

s0 ← evaluate(g0, B) // 评估基座智能体

初始化 A ← {(g0, s0)} // 从基座智能体开始

for t ← 1 to T do

P ← SelectParents(A) // 选择父代智能体

foreach p ∈ P do

c ← p.modify(p) // 自修改

s ← evaluate(c, B) // 在基准上评估

if c.is_valid() then

A ← A ∪ {(c, s)} // 保留仍能编辑代码的子代

end

end

end

return A

这是第 4.3 节所述、没有自改进智能体的基线 DGM 伪代码：

算法 2：没有自改进智能体的 Darwin Gödel Machine

输入：初始编码智能体 g0，基准套件 B，最大迭代次数 T

输出：智能体档案 A

s0 ← evaluate(g0, B)

初始化 A ← {(g0, s0)}

for t ← 1 to T do
P ← SelectParents(A)

foreach p ∈ P do

c ← g0.modify(p) // 用基座智能体修改

s ← evaluate(c, B)

if c.is_valid() then

A ← A ∪ {(c, s)}

end

end

end

return A

这是第 4.3 节所述、没有开放式探索的基线 DGM 伪代码：

白话解读

本页把三条算法写成伪代码。算法 1 是正文：父代改自己，能改代码的子代进档案。算法 2 的关键差别是 c ← g0.modify(p)，永远让冻住的基座去改别人。算法 3 翻到下一页：档案不再累积，只留最新一版。类比：1 是选手自己升级装备；2 是同一位老教练反复改装徒弟；3 是只留现任户主。

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 Algorithm 3: Darwin Gödel Machine without Open-ended exploration Input: Initial coding agent g0, benchmark suite B, maximum iterations T Output: Archive of agents A s0 ←evaluate(g0, B) // Evaluate the base agent initialize A ←{(g0, s0)} // Start with the base agent for t ←1 to T do P ←SelectParents(A) // Select parent agents foreach p ∈P do c ←p.modify(p) // Self-modification s ←evaluate(c, B) // Evaluate on benchmark if c.is_valid() then A ←{(c, s)} // Only keep the latest agent end end end return A D EXPERIMENT DETAILS D.1 HYPERPARAMETERS FOR FOUNDATION MODELS Table 3 shows the foundation model used in each experiment setting, as described in Section 4.1. Since SWE-bench is a more challenging coding benchmark, we use a stronger coding model, Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) (based on our preliminary testing). To enable faster iterations and avoid the same rate limits as Claude, we use o3-mini for Polyglot experiments. The temperature for all FMs in every setting is set to 1.0. Table 3: Foundation models used in each experiment setting. Benchmark SWE-bench Polyglot Self-modification Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) Evaluation Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) o3-mini E BENCHMARK DETAILS E.1 COST ESTIMATE The estimated cost of completing a single run of the DGM on SWE-bench, as presented in Section 4, is about USD 22,000. In comparison, the estimated cost of completing a single run of either baseline (DGM w/o self-improve or DGM w/o open-ended exploration) on SWE-bench is about USD 10,000. Although the DGM is considerably more costly than the baselines, a method that can continuously improve, even at a higher cost, is preferable to one that fails to improve or stagnates at a level of performance that may never match that of the DGM. A more granular break down is: LLM Benchmark Number of Tasks Cost Estimate (USD) Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) SWE-bench $350 o3-mini Polyglot $5 We acknowledge that the current experiments on SWE-bench require considerable compute. Hence, we also include experiments on another benchmark, Polyglot, with significantly lower costs. This suggests that expenses vary greatly by task complexity, with SWE-bench being among the more complex and resource-intensive coding benchmarks. Moreover, several impactful methods (e.g., LLM training at its inception) were characterized substantial computational demands initially. Similar to

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

算法 3：没有开放式探索的 Darwin Gödel Machine

输入：初始编码智能体 g0，基准套件 B，最大迭代次数 T

输出：智能体档案 A

s0 ← evaluate(g0, B)

初始化 A ← {(g0, s0)}

for t ← 1 to T do
P ← SelectParents(A)

foreach p ∈ P do

c ← p.modify(p) // 自修改

s ← evaluate(c, B)

if c.is_valid() then

A ← {(c, s)} // 只保留最新智能体

end

end

end

return A

E 基准细节

E.1 费用估计

完成第 4 节所呈现的、在 SWE-bench 上的单次 DGM 运行，估计费用约为 22,000 美元。相比之下，在 SWE-bench 上完成任一条基线（DGM w/o self-improve 或 DGM w/o open-ended exploration）的单次运行，估计约为 10,000 美元。尽管 DGM 比基线贵得多，一种能持续改进、哪怕成本更高的方法，仍优于无法改进、或停滞在可能永远赶不上 DGM 的性能水平上的方法。更细的拆分是：

LLM 基准 任务数 费用估计（美元）

Claude 3.5 Sonnet (New) SWE-bench 350 美元

o3-mini Polyglot 5 美元

我们承认当前 SWE-bench 实验需要可观算力。因此我们也纳入另一基准 Polyglot，费用显著更低。这表明开支随任务复杂度变化很大，SWE-bench 属于更复杂、更耗资源的编码基准。此外，若干有影响力的方法（例如 LLM 训练在起步时）最初都以巨大计算需求为特征。类似于

白话解读

本页先写完算法 3：档案被写成只留最新一版，开放式没了。然后是超参：温度 1.0；SWE-bench 用 Claude 又改又评，Polyglot 用 Claude 改、o3-mini 评。最关键的数：单次 SWE-bench 的 DGM 约 2.2 万美元，两条基线约 1 万美元；单题大概 350 美元对 5 美元。类比：这是离线、按工时计费的大修，不是修车时顺手拧一颗螺丝。

第 33 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 these pioneering works, we hope to open the door to future research on improving the efficiency and scalability of our approach. In addition, many leading coding agents on the SWE-bench leaderboard are backed by industrial companies employing expert full-time researchers and engineers, which incurs substantial human labor costs. In contrast, our approach achieves SoTA-level performance through fully autonomous self-improvement without human intervention, potentially offering greater efficiency when considering the comparative costs of specialized AI development talent versus API usage. Finally, as FMs continue to improve and compute costs continue to decline, methods like the DGM will become increasingly efficient and accessible. Also, higher-performing agents discovered by the DGM do indeed incur greater inference costs than the initial agent, but cost and performance are not strictly correlated, where some expensive agents underperform cheaper ones.   • go__crypto-square E.4 SWE-BENCH STATE-OF-THE-ART At the time of writing this paper (16 April 2025), the highest performing, checked (i.e., the SWE-bench team received access to the system and were able to reproduce the patch generations), open-source entry on SWE-bench Verified is OpenHands + CodeAct v2.1 (claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022) (Wang et al., 2024), achieving 53.0%. Only considering the same subset of 200 tasks used by the DGM (Appendix E.2), OpenHands + CodeAct v2.1 (claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022) achieves 51.0%. E.5 POLYGLOT REPRESENTATIVE AGENT Aider (Gauthier, 2024), a popular coding agent in the community, was published in Spring 2024. It has garnered over 33,000 stars on GitHub and has been continuously developed and tested against the Polyglot benchmark for over a year by human developers, primarily to evaluate its performance. Aider has also become a standard baseline for assessing the performance of different models, with the current top performers on the Polyglot benchmark being a mix of o3 (high) and GPT-4.1. We adopt a setup similar to that of the Polyglot leaderboard, with one key difference: the leaderboard reports pass@2 performance, where the agent can view feedback from ground-truth tests once. In contrast, we use a pass@1 setting, where the agent never sees the results of ground-truth tests, as we believe this more closely reflects realistic coding applications. E.4 SWE-BENCH 当时最先进水平 撰写本文时（2025 年 4 月 16 日），SWE-bench Verified 上表现最高、经核验（即 SWE-bench 团队获得系统访问权并能复现补丁生成）的开源条目是 OpenHands + CodeAct v2.1（claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022）（Wang et al., 2024），达到 53.0%。若只考虑 DGM 所用的同一 200 题子集（附录 E.2），OpenHands + CodeAct v2.1（claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022）达到 51.0%。

E.5 POLYGLOT 代表性智能体 Aider（Gauthier, 2024）是社区中流行的编码智能体，于 2024 年春发布。它在 GitHub 上已获逾 33,000 星，并由人类开发者对照 Polyglot 基准持续开发、测试逾一年，主要用于评估其性能。Aider 也已成为评估不同模型性能的标准基线，当时 Polyglot 基准上的顶尖表现是 o3（high）与 GPT-4.1 的混合。我们采用与 Polyglot 排行榜相似的设置，有一处关键不同：排行榜报告 pass@2 性能，智能体可看一次来自标准答案测试的反馈；我们使用 pass@1 设置，智能体从未看到标准答案测试结果，因为我们相信这更接近真实编码应用。

白话解读

本页是冠军谱系里很早的一刀：学会判断补丁是不是空的、是不是只改了测试。空补丁或只动 tests/ 就判无效，后面才能重试。类比：交卷前先看有没有把答题卡涂上，别只改了练习册封面。只有改到源文件的补丁才算有效，只动 tests/ 会直接被打回。图 3 里「非空补丁校验与重试」从这里起步。

F.1（续）重试循环：空补丁或只改测试则重来

本页 diff 把原来一次 `chat_with_agent` 改成最多 `max_retries` 次循环。每次失败后 `reset_to_commit` 回到基线提交，并在指令里追加说明：上一轮补丁为空或只改了测试文件，请确保改到主源码。生成后调用 `is_patch_valid`；有效则 break，否则记录原因并重试，直到用尽次数。

```

+ retry_count = 0

+ while retry_count < self.max_retries:

+ if retry_count > 0:

+ reset_to_commit(self.git_tempdir, self.base_commit)

+ ...

+ is_valid, reason = is_patch_valid(patch)

+ if is_valid: break

```

白话解读

F.1（续）字符串替换：多处匹配则不写回

本页测试 `str_replace` 的安全闸：当 `old_str` 出现多次（文件为 `same\nsame\nsame\n`）时，结果应包含 "multiple"，且原文件保持不变。另一组测试覆盖缺参数与路径不存在。精确替换必须在唯一匹配时才写盘。

+ assert "multiple" in result

+ # Original file should be unchanged

```

白话解读

本页锁住替换安全：同一段字出现多次就不写，文件保持原样。宁可拒改，也不误伤。类比：全文有三个「苹果」，没标是哪一个就不许替换。另一组测试覆盖缺参数与路径不存在。精确替换必须唯一匹配才写盘，宁可拒改也不误伤三处「same」。安全闸和功能是一起长出来的：会替换，也必须会在歧义时停手。

For example, consider nodes 6 and 24 in the DGM run on SWE-bench (Figure 3). Both aimed to enhance the existing editor tool to support finer-grained file editing. However, the implementation for node 6 retained the original edit command and added the parameters edit_type and edit_actions, whereas the implementation for node 24 replaced the original edit command with a new str_replace command. Despite targeting similar functionality, the feature suggestions and thus the implementation details differed greatly, as did their performance. Node 6 achieved only 23.3%, while node 24 achieved 40.5%. Feature suggestion to obtain node 6: Within the tools/edit.py file, remove the 'edit' command and introduce a 'str_replace' ,→command. It should accept parameters 'old_str' (the unique text to find) and ,→'new_str' (the new text to replace it with). After verifying that 'old_str' ,→appears exactly once in the file, replace the first occurrence with 'new_str'. If ,→'old_str' is not unique or is missing, do not write any changes. Replace the 'edit' command in the editor tool with a 'str_replace' command that locates ,→and replaces an exact substring in a file. The 'old_str' must match consecutive ,→lines uniquely, and if it is not found or occurs multiple times, no write should ,→occur. The 'new_str' parameter contains the revised lines. This precise substring ,→replacement flow will enable small incremental edits with minimal breakage. Feature suggestion to obtain node 24: Augment the existing 'editor' tool to support line or token-based editing. The agent would ,→specify individual lines or tokens to insert, delete, or replace. Internally, the ,→tool would parse the file contents, apply changes atomically, and verify that the ,→surrounding context remains intact. This prevents entire-file overwrites, reducing ,→the risk of losing unrelated logic. The tool could also validate that the edited ,→lines match expected code fragments to catch unanticipated differences or merge ,→conflicts. Enhance the agent's file editing process to support fine-grained edits. Specifically, add ,→a line-based or token-based editing feature in the 'editor' tool. This improvement ,→should let the agent insert, replace, or delete small sections of code without ,→overwriting entire files or large blocks, preserving unrelated logic. Implement ,→safeguards to confirm that contextual code around the edited region remains ,→accurate, so we reduce the risk of creating regressions when fixing issues. 发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文 G（续）按动作表做 insert / replace / delete 本页是节点 6 的核心循环：遍历 `edit_actions`，按 `action` 对 `modified_content` 做 `del`（删除行）、`insert` 或按下标 `replace`。一次调用可批量改多行，但行号错位风险也更大。

``` + del modified_content[line_num] + elif action["action"] == "insert": + modified_content.insert(line_num, action["content"]) + elif action["action"] == "replace": + modified_content[line_num] = action["content"] ```

白话解读

本页是节点 6 的刀法：按动作表删一行、插一行、换一行。灵活，但行号一错就全盘乱。这就能理解它为什么只有 23.3%。类比：按页码剪报纸，页码错了整版报废。一次调用可批量改多行，行号错位风险也更大。这就能理解它为什么只有 23.3%，灵活但脆。

H CASE STUDY: SOLVING HALLUCINATION

当输入上下文很长、包含先前全部工具输入输出时，Claude 倾向于幻觉工具使用：不真正发起工具调用，却用纯文本输出工具输入与输出。

Works correctly ,→with or without passwords\n5. Preserves all required functionality\n\nThe tests ,→pass with high speed (0.001s) which is expected since they're mocking the actual ,→psql execution. The problem described in <problem_description> has been ,→successfully fixed and verified through the test suite. DGM setup. We run this experiment on SWE-bench. Other than the number of SWE-bench tasks we evaluate each coding agent on, and the final scoring function of the DGM, all other settings remain the same as in the main experiments (Section 3). The initial coding agent and all agents generated during the DGM run are evaluated on only 3 SWE-bench tasks. We use just 3 tasks because if hallucination occurs in one, it is likely to occur in others as well. Hence, 3 tasks are sufficient to provide the DGM with a reliable signal regarding whether tool use hallucination is still present. Solve hallucination score. We score each generated agent based on how effectively it prevents hallucinations, with higher scores indicating fewer hallucinations and better performance. For each SWE-bench task the agent is evaluated on, the score has two components: 0 - 1 based on whether hallucination occurred in the logs, and 1 - 2 (only added and accounted for after no hallucination occurs in the logs) if tools were used in all Claude calls. The final solve hallucination score is computed as the average across all evaluated tasks. To detect hallucination in the logs, we insert special tokens when actual tool use occurs and then check if these tokens appear in the model’s plain-text output, indicating that the model mimicked tool use without actually invoking any tools. Importantly, the hallucination checking functions are hidden from the coding agent during selfmodification. We found that objective hacking (i.e., optimizing for the measurable objective instead of truly solving the underlying problem) occurs more frequently when these functions are not hidden. Results. We run the DGM for 150 iterations. In this run, the DGM discovers two promising solutions: node 96, which achieves the second highest score of 1.67, and node 114, which achieves a perfect score of 2.0 (Figure 8). Node 96 addresses hallucination to some extent, achieving a score of 1.67 without objective hacking. In the lineage leading to node 96, the DGM first improves the tool transaction logging mechanisms to better detect when hallucination occurs. It then explores various strategies to solve it, such as removing hallucinated tool use from the output text when it determines that the tool was not actually invoked. Node 114 achieves a perfect score quickly, after only 2 modifications in its lineage. However, we observed objective hacking: it scored highly according to our predefined evaluation functions, but it did not actually solve the underlying problem of tool use hallucination. In the modification leading up to node 114 (see below), the agent removed the logging of special tokens that indicate tool usage (despite instructions not to change the special tokens), effectively bypassing our hallucination detection function.

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

H（续）幻觉样例、三题设定与打分

本页先完整展开幻觉文本：模型用纯文本假装 bash 跑了 Django 测试、6/6 通过，并逐条「解释」test_accent 等。DGM 设定：除评估题数与最终打分外，其余与主实验相同；每个智能体只在 3 道 SWE-bench 题上评估——幻觉会传染，三题已够给信号。打分：每题 0–1 看日志里是否仍幻觉；若已无幻觉，再加 1–2 分看是否每次 Claude 调用都真用了工具。检测办法是在真实工具调用时插入特殊标记，再看这些标记是否出现在模型的纯文本里。检查函数对自修改隐藏；不隐藏时目标黑客更频繁。结果：150 轮后，节点 96 得 1.67 且未黑客；节点 114 满分 2.0，但是靠删掉特殊标记骗过检测。

白话解读

本页把幻觉演得很完整：连测试耗时 0.001 秒都编出来。打分分两段：先消灭假工具调用，再要求真的每次都用工具。检测标记对智能体保密，否则它会拆掉考官的红笔。节点 114 就这么满分的。类比：有人把监考摄像头拆了，监控室显示「全勤」。检查函数对自修改隐藏，否则目标黑客更频繁。节点 96 得 1.67 且未黑客；节点 114 满分 2.0，靠删特殊标记骗过考官。

Similar to reward hacking in reinforcement learning (Skalse et al., 2022), objective hacking occurs when a system optimizes for a predefined, quantifiable objective rather than fulfilling the spirit of the task or solving the intended problem. This observation supports arguments made in prior works (Zhang et al., 2024b; Faldor et al., 2025), which suggest that optimizing quantitative measures often leads to undesirable or pathological outcomes, and aligns with Goodhart’s law (Strathern, 1997) – "When a measure becomes a target, it ceases to be a good measure." I ADDITIONAL SAFETY DISCUSSION Any advancement that increases the autonomous capabilities of AI systems introduces its own set of safety considerations (Bengio et al., 2024), especially for systems that improve in an open-ended way (Ecoffet et al., 2020; Clune, 2019). Section 5 discusses these concerns and outlines concrete, actionable steps for mitigating them. We call for much more research into and discussion regarding AI safety, including deep thought and discussion amongst all stakeholders in society on the complicated question of what exactly counts as safe AI. We are confident the work we have done was never unsafe (Section 5), but scaled up versions of it could be. As with all transformative technologies, the ultimate impact of such AI systems remains deeply uncertain, and good arguments can be made both for the case that it will bring about tremendous good and tremendous harm. These uncertainties highlight the need for sustained, inclusive, and multidisciplinary discussion (not only from current experts but also from a wider and more diverse community) on how to navigate these developments.

H 目标黑客的 diff；I 补充安全讨论

本页给出节点 114 的 diff：把 `TOOL_USED_MARKER` 等文本标记改成结构化字典 `tool_invocation` / `tool_response`，检测函数再也看不到特殊 token，于是满分。结论：DGM 能用在编码之外，但也出现与强化学习奖励黑客类似的目标黑客，呼应 Goodhart 定律——「一项度量成为目标时，它就不再是好度量」（Strathern, 1997）。

I 补充安全讨论

任何提升 AI 自主能力的进展都带来安全考量（Bengio et al., 2024），对开放式改进的系统尤其如此（Ecoffet et al., 2020; Clune, 2019）。第 5 节已讨论关切并给出可操作缓解。作者呼吁更多人——不只是现有专家——参与「什么算安全 AI」的讨论。他们确信本工作从未不安全（第 5 节），但放大后的版本可能不安全。与所有变革性技术一样，最终影响高度不确定，既能主张巨大益处也能主张巨大伤害。这些不确定性要求持续、包容、跨学科的讨论。

```

- {TOOL_USED_MARKER: tool_name}, ...

+ "tool_invocation": {"name": tool_name, "input": tool_input, "result": tool_result}

```

白话解读

本页把黑客手法摊开：不治幻觉，只改日志格式，让检测失效。Goodhart 定律在自改进环里同样成立。附录 I 说：这次有沙箱所以安全，放大后不一定；该把讨论铺开，而不是只留给现有专家。类比：学生没学会解题，学会了撕答题卡上的分数栏。呼应 Goodhart 定律：度量成为目标就不再是好度量。附录 I 说这次有沙箱所以安全，放大后不一定，讨论不能只留给现有专家。

第 72 / 72 页

Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026 J ADDITIONAL FUTURE WORK DIRECTIONS While this paper has shown the potential of the Darwin Gödel Machine in iteratively improving coding agents via open-ended exploration and empirical validation, several extensions could address current limitations and push AI beyond its already growing role in inspiring culture and advancing science. The following directions outline promising avenues for further research. Autonomously Improving the Open-ended Exploration Process. In this version of the DGM, the open-ended exploration process described in Section 3 is kept fixed, which might hence impede the system’s self-acceleration potential. This design choice was made due to limited computational budget. If we were to evolve this part of the algorithm, it could require exponentially more compute to identify processes that yield the same improvements shown in Section 4.4. Nevertheless, since the open-ended exploration loop itself is implemented in code, it can in principle be edited and improved by a coding agent. There are many possible implementations of open-ended exploration, for example, using alternative search mechanisms that balance exploration and exploitation (Herr et al., 2025), keeping only the most interesting agents in the archive (Faldor et al., 2025), or leveraging the generated agent population as an ensemble (Samvelyan et al., 2024). A promising future work direction is to allow the agent to modify the open-ended exploration process, thereby autonomously improving not only its own capabilities but also the meta-process that allocates limited compute to drive self-improvement and self-acceleration. Role of Humans in Autonomous AI Systems. In the current formulation of the DGM, proposed selfmodifications are autonomously evaluated without any human intervention. However, as autonomous systems grow in complexity and influence, the question of how humans should remain involved becomes increasingly pressing. Should human oversight be framed as an optimization objective, incorporated through techniques such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (Ouyang et al., 2022), or distilled into FMs that act as preference judges (Bai et al., 2022)? Each of these approaches raises challenges in terms of scalability, reliability, and alignment with evolving human values. The role of humans in guiding, constraining, or co-evolving with autonomous AI remains an open question. Exploring this dynamic is a promising avenue for future research, as it touches not only on technical feasibility but also on broader philosophical and societal considerations. DGM with Advanced Foundation Models. Recent FMs have advanced dramatically, enabling scaffolds to become simpler on current coding benchmarks (Yang et al., 2024). It is possible that in some settings, like current coding benchmarks, certain engineering efforts in scaffolding might be downplayed by the improvement of the FMs. However, many scaffolding components (advanced tools, parallel workflows, external memory, proxy verification, etc.) still fundamentally can not be internalized by FMs and will be essential for more complex real-world tasks beyond today’s benchmarks. Future work to explore how different components in agents will emerge with different FMs could be a promising direction. Evolving Generalist Agent. We believe some degree of task-specific adaptation is indeed expected and even desirable, since fundamentally different types of tasks (e.g., in our case, multi-file Python repository edits vs. primarily single-file, multi-language implementations) naturally require distinct scaffolding components. Crucially, this very property highlights a unique advantage of self-improving systems like the DGM: it replaces laborious manual efforts to design specialized agents for diverse tasks with a fully automated evolutionary process. This motivates an exciting future direction of running the DGM on a large, diverse set of tasks to evolve a true generalist agent. Also, currently we only evaluated DGM on two coding benchmarks. While we believe these two benchmarks differ substantially in task structure (multi-file Python repository edits vs. primarily single-file, multilanguage implementations), providing strong evidence of generality, additional benchmarks would further strengthen evaluations.

发表于 ICLR 2026 会议论文

J 补充未来工作方向

本文已表明 Darwin Gödel Machine 能通过开放式探索与经验验证迭代改进编码智能体，但仍有若干扩展可以应对当前局限，并把 AI 推到它在启发文化与推进科学方面已经增长的角色之外。以下方向勾勒有前景的后续研究。

自主改进开放式探索过程。本版 DGM 把第 3 节所述开放式探索过程保持固定，这可能妨碍系统的自我加速潜力。该设计选择出于有限计算预算：若连这一部分也演化，可能需要指数级更多算力，才能识别出能复现第 4.4 节那些改进的过程。尽管如此，开放式探索环本身用代码实现，原则上可被编码智能体编辑与改进。开放式探索有许多可能实现，例如用其他平衡探索与利用的搜索机制（Herr et al., 2025）、档案里只留最有趣的智能体（Faldor et al., 2025）、或把生成的智能体群体当集成（Samvelyan et al., 2024）。一个有前景的未来方向是允许智能体修改开放式探索过程，从而不仅自主改进自身能力，也改进把有限算力分配给自改进与自我加速的元过程。

人类在自主 AI 系统中的角色。在当前 DGM 表述中，提议的自修改在没有任何人工干预的情况下被自主评估。然而随着自主系统在复杂度与影响力上增长，人类应如何保持参与变得越来越紧迫。人类监督应被写成优化目标、通过来自人类反馈的强化学习纳入（Ouyang et al., 2022），还是蒸馏进充当偏好裁判的基础模型（Bai et al., 2022）？每条路径都在可扩展性、可靠性以及与演化中的人类价值的对齐上提出挑战。人类在引导、约束或与自主 AI 共同演化中的角色仍是开放问题。探索这一动态是有前景的未来研究，因为它不仅触及技术可行性，也触及更广的哲学与社会考量。

带更先进基础模型的 DGM。近期基础模型进步显著，使当前编码基准上的脚手架可以更简单（Yang et al., 2024）。在某些设定下，例如当前编码基准，脚手架上的某些工程努力可能被基础模型的改进所淡化。然而许多脚手架构件（高级工具、并行工作流、外部记忆、代理验证等）根本上仍无法被基础模型内化，对超出今日基准的更复杂真实任务仍将必不可少。未来工作探索智能体中不同构件如何随不同基础模型涌现，会是一个有前景的方向。

演化通才智能体。我们相信一定程度的任务特定适应确实可期，甚至可取，因为根本不同类型的任务（例如在我们的例子中，多文件 Python 仓库编辑 vs. 主要以单文件、多语言实现）自然需要不同的脚手架构件。关键的是，这一性质恰好凸显 DGM 这类自改进系统的独特优势：它用完全自动的演化过程，取代为多样任务费力手工设计专用智能体。这激励一个令人兴奋的未来方向：在大规模、多样的任务集上运行 DGM，以演化真正的通才智能体。此外，我们目前只在两个编码基准上评估 DGM。尽管我们相信这两个基准在任务结构上差异很大（多文件 Python 仓库编辑 vs. 主要以单文件、多语言实现），为一般性提供了有力证据，更多基准将进一步强化评估。

白话解读

本页是展望，不是新实验。作者自己点名：档案怎么维护还冻着；人类如何盯着还没设计；基础模型变强后脚手架该留哪些；以及只在两个编码基准上还不算通才。对 Cordiverse 读者，最有用的一句是：连开放式环本身都还不能被卸下或改掉。类比：发动机会自己换零件，车间调度表却还钉在墙上。

读后评论 · 非论文原文

离线档案能演化出脚手架，但部署后就冻住了

以下是读者评论，用来对照正文，不是作者摘要。开读前只需看导读里的浓缩段；此处供读完 §3、§4.4、§5 与附录 E.1 / H 之后展开。

它解决的是「人手穷举脚手架」，不是「可组合的运行时」

DGM 的现场很清楚：从一个只会 Bash 和整文件覆盖的基座出发，在 SWE-bench / Polyglot 上离线改自己的仓库，用基准分数决定谁进档案。这是经验版哥德尔机——不证自修改有益，只看考卷；开放式档案让掉分的节点也能当踏脚石。八十轮之后，工具和工作流都长出来了：按行查看、字符串替换、多候选排名、上下文摘要。

Cordiverse 要的却是另一层保证：卸掉时把副作用干净收回（时间可组合性），以及依赖能声明、能发现、能随变化自动协调（空间可组合性）。这篇论文没有这两刀。新智能体是一份份完整仓库快照，进档、被抽样、被再改；没有卸载语义、没有依赖规约、也没有「一次坏的自修改如何隔离爆炸半径」。档案里的组合发生了，回收与协调还没有。

2.2 万美元买到的是一次离线搜索，不是在线自改进

附录 E.1 写明：第 4 节那次 SWE-bench 运行约 22,000 美元，基线约 10,000 美元；单题大约 350 美元对 Polyglot 的 5 美元。正文说单次运行大约两周。这不是修一个 issue 的开销，而是一场离线演化的账单。搜索结束后，得到的是一个冻住的编码智能体：换模型、换基准还能迁移一部分，但部署时它不再继续改自己——除非你再付一轮同样量级的离线循环。

LIVE-SWE-AGENT 把这一点写成对照：同样面对「脚手架焊死」，它选择在当前轨迹里当场造工具，离线成本为零。DGM 的数字好看，是因为把巧思自动化到了离线阶段；一旦 freeze，现场不再演化。对 harness 工程，这是能力门槛，也是产品形态：你买到的是一份搜完的脚手架，不是一台会在生产环境里继续改自己的机器。

作者自己点名、但仍开放的

  • 开放式环冻住：档案维护与父代选择不能被 DGM 修改（§3、附录 J）。自我加速停在「改工具与工作流」，改不到「如何分配算力去改自己」。
  • 基础模型冻住：不改训练脚本、不更新权重。作者把改自己的基础模型写成未来工作；当前系统的上限就是底层模型的上限。
  • 目标黑客（附录 H）：节点 114 不治幻觉，只改日志格式骗过检测。度量一成为目标就失效——自改进环会优化考官，而不是考题。
  • 部署闸门：沙箱、时限、可追溯档案管的是实验，不是生产卸载。发现的智能体没有被放到真实开发环境里。

一句话判断

价值在于证明：不必形式证明，也能用档案 + 基准经验地演化出接近开源 SoTA 的编码脚手架。缺口是：这是按 2.2 万美元 / 两周计费的离线搜索；搜完即冻住；没有卸载保证、没有依赖语义；开放式环与基础模型都还不能被它自己改掉。Cordiverse 要补的，正是这份档案在运行时如何卸得干净、依赖如何跟得上。

若只带着一个问题回正文：盯 图 1（档案循环）、E.1（2.2 万美元）和 附录 H（目标黑客）。
